### [44] [Perturbative-NeuSA: A Structured Spectral Framework for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24345)
*Xianli Zhu,Jia Yin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Perturbative-NeuSA残差框架，将神经谱求解器分解为低保真背景与高分辨率微扰，仅学习未解析动力学。在2D Burgers、Klein-Gordon和异构波动方程上，确定性结构化求解器无需训练即超越有训练的NeuSA基线，而神经闭包的效果具有条件性：仅当背景质量差、残差有结构且与闭包模型兼容时才有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经谱PDE求解器常学习整个未解析向量场，但廉价的近似模型已能捕获大部分轨迹，导致大量计算资源浪费在已经可预测的部分上。现有方法缺乏对物理结构与神经网络各自贡献的分离测量机制。

Method: 将目标解分解为低保真背景与高分辨率微扰，从精确微扰方程出发，结合固定谱算子、背景依赖校正、目标PDE中的背景缺陷以及可选神经闭包，构建残差公式。仅学习未解析的动力学部分。

Result: Burgers方程上确定性校正将训练和外推误差分别降低24倍和44倍；Klein-Gordon扫过7种背景分辨率发现闭包在差背景上提升3.6倍，中等分辨率中性，好背景反而退化；波动方程上闭包在界面局部化残差上额外降低18%。多初值诊断显示闭包有效区域取决于初始条件谱。

Conclusion: 重定义了神经闭包为一种有条件的、可诊断的校正，其有效性由背景保真度、残差组织方式以及与闭包模型的兼容性共同决定。Perturbative-NeuSA框架使得物理结构与神经闭包的贡献可被分别测量。

Abstract: Neural spectral PDE solvers often learn an entire unresolved vector field even when an inexpensive approximate model can already capture most of the trajectory. Here we introduce Perturbative-NeuSA, a residual formulation that decomposes the target solution into a low-fidelity background and a high-resolution perturbation, so that only the unresolved dynamics is learned. Starting from the exact perturbation equation, the method combines a fixed spectral operator, a background-dependent correction, the background defect in the target PDE, and an optional neural closure. This construction makes the roles of physical structure and neural closure separately measurable. Across 2D Burgers, Klein-Gordon, and heterogeneous 2D wave equations, the deterministic structured solver outperforms the trained NeuSA baseline while requiring no neural-network training. The largest gains occur on Burgers, where the deterministic correction reduces training and extrapolation errors by factors of 24 and 44, respectively. In addition, a Klein-Gordon sweep over seven background resolutions shows that the effect of the closure is conditional: it improves a poor background by 3.6 times, becomes neutral at intermediate resolutions, and degrades a well-resolved background. For the wave equation, however, the closure provides an additional 18% reduction when the remaining residual is interface-localized. Multi-initial-condition diagnostics further show that the useful closure regime depends on the initial-condition spectrum and can disappear in extrapolation when structured correction already captures the dominant Burgers dynamics. Perturbative-NeuSA therefore reframes neural closure as a conditional, diagnosable correction governed by background fidelity, residual organization, and compatibility with the closure model.

</details>


### [45] [Semalith v1.4: A Calibrated 184M Safety Classifier Achieving State-of-the-Art Prompt-Injection Detection at 44x Fewer Parameters than Llama-Guard-3-8B](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22545)
*Tejasvi C. Addagada*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Semalith v1.4 是一个184M参数的DeBERTa-v3-base分类器，可在单次前向传播中同时处理提示注入、通用危害和金融合规三个轴线的安全分类，在22个基准测试中的21个实现零污染，以44倍更少的参数在提示注入评估中全面超越Llama-Guard-3-8B。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在金融服务和智能体场景中部署大语言模型需要同时处理提示注入、监管合规和通用危害的安全分类器，而现有开源防护方案无法在一次推理中同时满足这些需求。

Method: 基于DeBERTa-v3-base架构的184M参数分类器，采用22类分类头（BENIGN、9种提示注入子类型、通用危害、11种BFSI标签）和4类辅助超类头，使用联合加权损失训练。数据集为从49个公开来源挖掘的76,204行语料，通过SHA-1去重确保与所有保留评估集零污染（21/22基准零污染，最大0.22%）。

Result: 在22个保留基准测试中，Semalith v1.4以44倍更少的参数在全部7个提示注入评估中胜出，整体18个基准中赢得11个。在208个良性智能体提示上FPR为0.000（Llama-Guard-3-8B为0.063）。在通用危害基准上Llama-Guard-3领先，形成互补性分工。

Conclusion: v1.3推荐用于对话审核部署（ToxicChat F1 0.624），v1.4推荐用于需要BFSI标签覆盖或良性智能体提示零FPR的场景。披露了6个已测量的弱点。

Abstract: Deploying large language models in financial-services and agentic settings requires safety classifiers that simultaneously handle prompt injection, regulatory compliance, and general harm, a combination no existing open guardrail addresses in a single inference pass.
  Semalith v1.4 is a 184M-parameter DeBERTa-v3-base classifier performing simultaneous three-axis safety classification including prompt injection, general harm, and financial-services regulatory compliance, in a single forward pass. Its 22-class head (BENIGN, nine prompt-injection sub-types, general-harm, eleven BFSI labels) is trained with a 4-class auxiliary super-category head under jointly weighted loss, on a 76,204-row corpus mined from 49 public sources with SHA-1 deduplication against every held-out evaluation set, with 21 of 22 benchmarks at zero contamination (max 0.22%).
  Against Llama-Guard-3-8B on 22 held-out benchmarks, Semalith v1.4 wins every prompt-injection evaluation (7/7) and 11 of 18 benchmarks overall at 44x fewer parameters, with FPR = 0.000 on 208 benign agentic prompts vs 0.063 for Llama-Guard-3-8B. On general-harm benchmarks (WildGuardMix, HEx-PHI, HarmBench), Llama-Guard-3 leads; this complementary split is documented in Section 4. Six measured weak spots are disclosed in Section 6.
  Deployment guidance: v1.3 is recommended for conversational moderation deployments (ToxicChat F1 0.624); v1.4 is recommended when BFSI label coverage or zero-FPR on benign agentic prompts is the priority.

</details>


### [46] [Low-Rank Dependence Decomposition via Accelerated Symmetric Non-negative Matrix Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24518)
*Lavinia Ghita,Dhruv Desai,Jake Goldberg,Roman Yokunda Enzmann*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于迹恒等式重写的SymNMF方法，消除了O(n²)内存需求，实现了单GPU处理n≈10⁵、多节点扩展到n=10⁶的大规模非负矩阵分解。在两大矩阵类型（Pearson相关和尾部相依）上比较了7个算法族（30+配置），发现AdaGrad系列和Block-SVRG AdaptGrow在不同谱结构下各有优势，并揭示了球面K-means在单因子退化情况下的局限性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对称非负矩阵分解(SymNMF)在恢复依赖矩阵中的潜在群结构方面具有重要价值，但传统方法因存储和计算O(n²)的密集矩阵而无法扩展到大规模（n超过数万）。随着金融投资组合风险管理中风险因子估计的需求日益增长，亟需一种能够处理n≈10⁵乃至10⁶量级的大规模SymNMF方法。

Method: (1) 提出迹恒等式重写目标函数，消除所有n×n中间矩阵的显式构造，大幅降低内存占用；(2) 设计两阶段收敛协议，系统比较7个算法族（梯度下降、AdaGrad、RMSprop、Adam、ADMM、坐标下降、SVRG变体等）共30+配置；(3) 引入三种新方法：Piecewise AdaGrad、Row-Stochastic SVRG、Block-SVRG AdaptGrow；(4) 在绝对Pearson相关矩阵（主导低秩谱）和尾部尾部相依矩阵（平坦病态谱）两类代理矩阵上进行大规模GPU实验。

Result: (1) 在中等规模下，11种方法可以收敛，其中6种在n=10⁵时仍保持高效（5种AdaGrad系列+ADMM），5种AdaGrad系列方法在n=10⁶时仍能收敛；(2) 最快的求解器追踪矩阵谱结构：Block-SVRG AdaptGrow在平坦病态的尾部相依谱上获胜（每次迭代成本低）；全批量AdaGrad在主导低秩的相关谱上获胜（运行时间短）；(3) 球面K-means在存在角度聚类结构时更便宜，但当矩阵向单一公共因子退化时出现退化，此时软分解仍然是必要的。

Conclusion: 本文通过迹恒等式重写成功将SymNMF扩展到n=10⁶量级，验证了不同谱结构需要不同的优化器策略：病态平坦谱适合随机方差缩减方法（Block-SVRG AdaptGrow），而低秩谱适合全批量自适应方法（AdaGrad）。这为大规模金融风险因子分解提供了实用的算法选择和理论指导。

Abstract: Symmetric non-negative matrix factorization (SymNMF) recovers latent group structure from a dependence matrix, but its dense, quadratic-memory objective has confined prior work to moderate sizes. We present a large-scale GPU study of seven algorithm families (over 30 configurations) on absolute Pearson correlation and tail pairwise dependence matrices from Extreme Value Theory, two proxies for empirical risk-factor estimation on large portfolios. A trace-identity reformulation eliminates all $n \times n$ intermediates, so a single GPU reaches $n \approx 10^5$ and multi-node distribution scales to $n = 10^6$ and beyond. Under a two-phase protocol, eleven methods converge at moderate scale; six remain efficient enough at $n = 10^5$ (five AdaGrad-family plus ADMM), and five AdaGrad-family methods still converge at $n = 10^6$: AdaGrad, RMSprop, and three we introduce (Piecewise AdaGrad, Row-Stochastic SVRG, Block-SVRG AdaptGrow). At $n = 10^6$ the fastest solver tracks the matrix spectrum: Block-SVRG AdaptGrow wins on the flat, ill-conditioned tail-dependence spectrum, where its lower per-iteration cost decides a long factorization, and full-batch AdaGrad wins on the dominant-low-rank correlation spectrum, where the run is short. We also benchmark spherical K-means as a hard-label baseline: cheaper when angular cluster structure is present, yet provably degenerate once the matrix collapses toward a single common factor, where the soft factorization remains necessary.

</details>


### [47] [CORVUS: Context Optimization and Reduction Via Underlying Synchronization for LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22711)
*Mingwei Zheng,David OBrien,Siwei Cui,Pardis Pashakhanloo,Rajdeep Mukherjee,Myeongsoo Kim,Sachit Kuhar*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CORVUS是一种新型轨迹架构，通过解耦文件读取操作与观测结果，维护文件同步注册表，在每次推理循环中注入文件当前内容，从而消除冗余和过时快照，使LLM编码代理的轨迹更轻量、与代码库状态同步，在SWE-BENCH基准上实现9-50%的token减少、15-32%最终提示缩短和37%推理循环减少，同时保持相当的通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM编码代理采用追加式轨迹架构，将文件读取动作与观测结果紧密耦合，导致快照在文件变更后过时，引发推理错误和冗余重读，每个重读又追加新副本到轨迹中，造成轨迹臃肿和状态不同步。

Method: 提出CORVUS架构，核心创新是解耦文件读取动作与观测结果：维护一个相关文件的同步注册表，在每个推理循环中仅注入文件当前内容，而非将完整快照永久固定在轨迹历史中。

Result: 在SWE-POLYBENCH_VERIFIED和SWE-BENCH PRO上评估四种LLM，实现每个任务输入token减少9-50%，最终提示缩短15-32%，推理循环减少多达37%，同时保持可比的通过率。

Conclusion: CORVUS通过结构性改变轨迹架构，有效解决了传统追加式轨迹中快照过时和冗余副本的问题，显著降低了token消耗和推理开销，同时不影响任务完成质量。

Abstract: LLM coding agents operate by constructing trajectories that accumulate reasoning, tool calls, and results to enable multi-step decision-making. However, the conventional append-only trajectory architecture found in practice tightly couples file-read actions with their observations, capturing snapshots that become permanently fixed in the chronological history. As files change through agent edits or concurrent human modifications, these snapshots become stale, causing reasoning errors and causing agents to redundantly re-read files, with each re-read appending yet another copy to the trajectory. To mitigate this, we propose CORVUS, a novel trajectory architecture that decouples file-read actions from their observations by maintaining a synchronized registry of relevant files and injecting only their current contents at each reasoning cycle. This structural change produces significantly lighter-weight trajectories that remain synchronized with the actual codebase state by construction, eliminating redundant file copies and stale snapshots that bloat conventional trajectories. We evaluated CORVUS on SWE- POLYBENCH_VERIFIED and SWE-BENCH PRO across four LLMs, achieving 9-50% reduction in average input tokens per task, 15-32% shorter final prompts, and up to 37% fewer reasoning cycles while maintaining comparable pass rates.

</details>


### [48] [Global Convergence of DGM and PINN Algorithms for Solving Nonlinear PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24726)
*Justin Sirignano,Konstantinos Spiliopoulos,Samuel Cohen*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文针对DGM和PINNs方法，证明了一类半线性PDE的神经网络在梯度下降训练下能收敛到PDE解，解决了该领域长期存在的数学基础问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: DGM和PINNs方法虽已广泛应用，但由于PDE残差目标函数的非凸性，训练后的神经网络可能仅收敛到局部最小值而非PDE真实解，因此需要从数学上证明算法能收敛到PDE解，建立其数学基础。

Method: 针对一类半线性PDE（解及其一阶导数非线性），通过理论分析证明神经网络使用梯度下降最小化PDE残差目标函数时，训练过程会收敛到PDE的真实解。

Result: 对于所考虑的半线性PDE类别，证明了神经网络在梯度下降训练下确实收敛到PDE的解，而非仅仅是局部极小值。

Conclusion: 该工作为DGM和PINNs方法提供了严格的数学基础保障，表明至少在所研究的一类半线性PDE中，这些方法的收敛性是有理论保证的。

Abstract: The Deep Galerkin Method (DGM) and Physics Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) have become widely-used methods for solving partial differential equations (PDEs) in the rapidly growing field of scientific machine learning. In these methods, a neural network is trained to approximate the PDE solution by using (stochastic) gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual of the neural network. Due to the non-convexity of the PDE residual objective function, the trained neural network may, in principle, only converge to a local minimizer of the objective function (which would not be a solution of the PDE). Therefore, there is a longstanding question regarding the mathematical foundations of these algorithms, and it is highly valuable to establish that the trained neural network will converge to the PDE solution. For a class of semi-linear PDEs (nonlinear in the solution and its first derivative), we prove that neural networks trained with gradient descent to minimize the PDE residual objective function will converge to the PDE solution.

</details>


### [49] [CausalGate: Causal Importance Distillation for Transformer Module Pruning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22720)
*Kiran Nair,Smriti Regmi,Rodrigue Rizk*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: CausalGate提出了一种基于因果干预的框架，通过校准阶段精确测量Attention和MLP子层对最终语义的因果贡献，并将重要性层级蒸馏为静态轻量级标量门控，实现零运行时开销的高效Transformer推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自适应推理方法依赖隐藏状态相似性或激活幅度等观测启发式来丢弃冗余模块，但这些基于相关性的度量无法捕捉对语义准确性至关重要的非线性结构计算，导致推理效率与精度之间的权衡不佳。

Method: 在校准阶段，CausalGate通过单独隔离每个Attention和MLP子层、将其输出置零，并利用最终logit分布的KL散度精确测量语义损伤；然后将结构重要性层级通过指数移动平均平滑目标和可微分的成对排序损失蒸馏为一组静态轻量级标量门控，消除运行时路由开销。

Result: 在TinyLlama-1.1B、Qwen2.5-3B和Llama-3.1-8B上，针对语言建模和常识推理基准测试，CausalGate在性能上持续优于主流的动态路由和跳层基线方法，并将理论计算节省转化为实际的硬件延迟降低，且零运行开销。

Conclusion: 基于因果干预而非相关性启发式来评估模块重要性，能够更准确地识别冗余计算，同时通过蒸馏为静态门控避免了动态路由的额外开销，为高效Transformer推理提供了一种更可靠的范式。

Abstract: Existing adaptive inference methods for Large Language Models rely on observational heuristics, such as hidden-state similarity or activation magnitudes, to drop redundant modules. However, these correlation-based metrics often fail to capture subtle, non-linear structural computations vital for semantic accuracy. We introduce CausalGate, an intervention-guided framework for compute-efficient transformer inference. During a calibration phase, CausalGate isolates individual Attention and MLP sub-layers, zeros out their respective outputs, and measures the exact semantic damage via the Kullback-Leibler divergence of the final logit distribution. To eliminate runtime routing overhead, this structural importance hierarchy is distilled into a global set of static, lightweight scalar gates using an Exponential Moving Average smoothing objective paired with a differentiable pairwise ranking loss. Evaluated on TinyLlama-1.1B, Qwen2.5-3B, and Llama-3.1-8B across language modeling and commonsense reasoning benchmarks, CausalGate consistently outperforms prominent dynamic routing and layer-skipping baselines, translating theoretical compute savings into concrete hardware latency reductions with zero operational overhead.

</details>


### [50] [Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization for Long-Horizon Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22724)
*Kaibing Yang,Guangfeng Cai,Shengtian Yang,Shuo He,Yu Li,Mengyi Liu,Pengwei Chen,Jun Xu,Lei Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对组策略优化在长周期任务中因采样不平衡导致的信用分配陷阱（失败样本主导、缺乏有效奖励信号），提出ProGPO方法，当组内所有样本均获得零奖励时，基于首次访问的观测覆盖率为轨迹/步骤分配相对优势，以促进探索新状态。在ALFWorld和WebShop基准上，ProGPO在困难任务上显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有组策略优化方法在困难长周期任务中存在采样不平衡问题：重复或低效动作占据策略高概率区域，而有效状态改变动作采样不足，导致大量全失败轨迹组，无法提供有效奖励信号引导策略修正，形成自我强化的信用分配陷阱。

Method: 提出Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO)，核心创新为：仅在组内所有样本均获得零结果奖励时，基于首次访问观测覆盖率为轨迹或步骤分配更高的相对优势，因为到达新状态是任务成功的前提条件。

Result: 在ALFWorld和WebShop两个具有挑战性的智能体基准上，使用Qwen2.5-1.5B/7B-Instruct模型进行实验，ProGPO在组策略优化基线方法上取得一致改进，在困难任务上提升尤为显著。

Conclusion: ProGPO通过利用观测覆盖率作为零奖励场景下的代理奖励信号，有效打破了失败主导的信用分配陷阱，提升了长周期智能体任务的策略学习效率。

Abstract: Group-based policy optimization has been increasingly used to train large language model (LLM) agents from sparse outcome rewards by comparing trajectories or steps within a group. However, on difficult long-horizon tasks, this comparison can suffer from a sampling imbalance: repeated or low-effect actions dominate the high-probability region of the policy while useful state-changing actions remain under-sampled. This imbalance produces many all-failed rollout groups, where outcome rewards provide no direction for correcting the policy. Together, these effects can form a self-reinforcing credit trap: failure-dominated sampling yields no outcome-based correction, allowing repeated low-effect actions to persist. To break this loop, we propose Progress-conditioned Group Policy Optimization (ProGPO), which uses first-visit observation coverage only when all samples in a group receive zero outcome reward. Specifically, within such groups, ProGPO assigns higher relative advantages to trajectories or steps that visit more new states since reaching new observations is a prerequisite for task success. Experiments on two challenging agentic benchmarks, ALFWorld and WebShop with Qwen2.5-1.5/7B-Instruct, show that ProGPO consistently improves over group-based baselines, with particularly large gains on hard tasks.

</details>


### [51] [Bitcoin Price Direction Prediction via Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Fusion of Social Sentiment and Technical Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23370)
*Muhammad Abdullah Haroon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Learning (RAML)，通过动态检测市场状态（稳定/波动）自适应融合比特币价格特征与社交情绪特征，在3小时和6小时预测任务上优于传统静态拼接方法，并验证了自适应融合是多模态金融预测的必要设计原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统多模态金融预测方法对价格特征和社交情绪特征采用静态拼接融合，忽略了行为金融学中“零售情绪在波动期更具预测力、在平静期更具噪声”的发现，缺乏对市场状态的感知能力。

Method: RAML使用滚动24小时波动率将观测数据划分为稳定/波动两种市场状态；设计可学习的sigmoid门控机制，根据检测到的市场状态动态调整情绪嵌入与价格嵌入的融合权重——在波动期更信任情绪特征，在稳定期更信任价格特征。

Result: 在3491小时数据上，RAML在3小时和6小时预测中宏F1分别达0.5474和0.5513，3小时AUC为0.5084；消融实验表明每个组件均不可或缺，用静态拼接替换自适应融合导致6小时召回率崩溃（F1降至0.14）。

Conclusion: 基于市场状态的条件化自适应融合是多模态金融预测的必要设计原则，RAML验证了行为金融学洞见可有效指导深度学习模型架构设计。

Abstract: Bitcoin price prediction on sub-daily timescales is a hard open problem in computational finance. Bitcoin exhibits fat-tailed returns, non-stationary dynamics, and a price discovery process influenced by social discourse on Reddit and Twitter. Conventional approaches fuse OHLCV technical features with sentiment via static concatenation, applying identical fusion weights regardless of market state. This is inconsistent with the behavioural finance literature, which shows that retail sentiment is most predictive during volatile periods and noisy during calm ones. This paper proposes Regime-Aware Multi-Modal Learning (RAML), which conditions fusion of sentiment and price features on a dynamically detected binary market regime. Rolling 24-hour volatility partitions observations into stable and volatile regimes; a learnable sigmoid gate adjusts the weight of the sentiment embedding relative to the price embedding, trusting sentiment more during volatility and price dynamics more during stable phases. The system is evaluated on 3,491 hourly observations (July 2024-September 2025), combining Bitcoin OHLCV data with Reddit /r/Bitcoin FinBERT sentiment. Four models are compared - price-only BiLSTM, sentiment-only classifier, static-concatenation BiLSTM, and RAML - across 3-hour and 6-hour horizons, with an ablation study isolating the sentiment branch, regime detection, and adaptive fusion. RAML achieves macro-F1 of 0.5474 (3h) and 0.5513 (6h), with the highest AUC at 3 hours (0.5084), indicating better calibration. Ablation confirms every component is necessary, and replacing adaptive weighting with concatenation causes recall collapse at 6 hours (F1: 0.14). These results establish regime-conditioned adaptive fusion as a necessary design principle for multi-modal financial forecasting.

</details>


### [52] [QFedPolyp: A Communication- and Inference-Efficient Federated Learning Framework for Polyp Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22743)
*Madan Baduwal,Priyanka Paudel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: QFedPolyp是一个结合量化感知训练与低精度模型通信的联邦学习框架，用于隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，在保持分割精度的同时将通信成本降低约4倍，推理速度提升最多1.5倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 集中式深度学习需要医院共享敏感医疗数据，存在隐私风险；联邦学习虽能保护隐私，但全精度模型参数的反复传输导致通信成本过高。因此需要一种通信高效且推理高效的联邦学习方案，用于协作式息肉分割。

Method: QFedPolyp将量化感知训练（Quantization-Aware Training）与低精度模型通信相结合。每家医院在私有数据上本地训练轻量级U-Net，同时在前向/反向传播中模拟量化效果。客户端传输量化后的模型参数至中央服务器，服务器使用联邦平均（Federated Averaging）重构并聚合参数。

Result: 全精度联邦训练在Kvasir-SEG上达到0.910 Dice，在CVC-ClinicVideoDB上达到0.930 Dice。均匀8-bit通信将传输成本降低约4倍，同时保持竞争性的分割精度。量化模型推理速度比全精度模型提升最多1.5倍。

Conclusion: QFedPolyp实现了隐私保护的协作式息肉分割，降低了通信开销并加速了推理，得到的轻量化模型适用于实时临床部署。

Abstract: Background and Objective: Automatic polyp segmentation supports computer-aided diagnosis and early colorectal cancer detec- tion. Centralized deep learning requires hospitals to share sensitive medical data, while federated learning preserves privacy but introduces high communication costs through repeated transmission of full-precision model parameters. We propose QFedPolyp, a communication- and inference-efficient federated learning framework for collaborative polyp segmentation.
  Methods: QFedPolyp combines quantization-aware training with low-precision model communication. Each hospital locally trains a lightweight U-Net on private data while simulating quantization during training. Clients transmit quantized model parameters to a central server, where they are reconstructed and aggregated using Federated Averaging. Evaluation is performed on Kvasir-SEG, CVC-ClinicVideoDB, PolypGen, and BKAI-IGH NeoPolyp.
  Results: Full-precision federated training achieves Dice scores of 0.910 on Kvasir-SEG and 0.930 on CVC-ClinicVideoDB. Uni- form 8-bit communication reduces transmission cost by approximately 4 times while preserving competitive segmentation accuracy. Quantized models also achieve up to 1.5 times faster inference than full-precision models.
  Conclusions: QFedPolyp enables privacy-preserving collaborative polyp segmentation with reduced communication overhead and faster inference. The resulting lightweight models are suitable for real-time clinical deployment.

</details>


### [53] [MAPLE: Efficient and Diverse Multi-Alpha Generation for Portfolio Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24131)
*Yu-Chen Den,Kuan-Yu Chen,Kendro Vincent,Tien-Hao Chang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: MAPLE提出了一种无需修改骨干网络的多Alpha生成框架，通过统一预测头、极端排名加权损失和多样性正则化，在美股、中国和日本四个市场上用更少参数和训练时间实现了更好的夏普比率和卡尔马比率，证明合理的损失设计和容量分配比复杂的架构更重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典alpha挖掘通过组合低相关性的预测信号实现强风险调整收益，但深度学习方法通常每个股票只产生单一alpha，依赖日益复杂的架构收益递减，多样性仅通过分离模型或隐式路由获得，缺乏对Alpha间相关性的显式控制。

Method: MAPLE是一个骨干无关的框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) 统一的可扩展容量预测头；(2) 极端排名加权的列表级排名损失；(3) 显式惩罚Alpha间成对相关性的多样性正则化器。在单个训练过程中同时优化。

Result: 在美、中、日四个股票市场上，MAPLE在9个基线中取得最佳平均夏普比率和卡尔马比率，参数减少55倍，训练时间减少2.5倍；在5种骨干架构上夏普比率提升10-23%，卡尔马比率提升17-43%。

Conclusion: 合理的损失函数设计和容量分配，而非架构复杂性，是驱动多样化和有效多Alpha生成的关键。统一头在应用任何多样性损失前已降低Alpha间相关性，极端排名损失使多样性正则化能够改善而非侵蚀每个Alpha的排名质量。

Abstract: Classical alpha mining achieves strong risk-adjusted returns by combining many low-correlated predictive signals, yet deep learning stock-ranking methods typically produce a single alpha per stock, rely on increasingly complex architectures with diminishing gains, and obtain diversity only through separate models or implicit routing, without explicitly controlling inter-alpha correlation. We introduce MAPLE (Multi-Alpha Position-aware Listwise Ensembling), a backbone-agnostic framework that recovers this diversity principle within a single training pass. MAPLE combines a unified, capacity-scaled prediction head with an extreme-rank weighted listwise ranking loss and a diversity regularizer that explicitly penalizes pairwise correlation across alphas. Across four equity markets spanning the US, China, and Japan, MAPLE achieves the best average Sharpe and Calmar ratios among nine baselines, using up to 55x fewer parameters and 2.5x less training time, and generalizes across five backbone architectures with Sharpe and Calmar Ratio gains of 10-23% and 17-43%, respectively. Behavioral analysis further shows why each component works: the unified head already reduces inter-alpha correlation before any diversity loss is applied, and the extreme-rank loss lets diversity regularization improve rather than erode per-alpha ranking quality as capacity scaling sustains this balance at scale. These results show that principled loss design and capacity allocation, rather than architectural complexity, drive diverse and effective multi-alpha generation.

</details>


### [54] [Learning to Access Computation: Accessibility Plasticity as a Principle of Adaptive Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22748)
*Zhaowen Fan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出"可访问性可塑性"（Accessibility Plasticity）作为神经网络适应性的一个新维度，区别于传统的计算能力修改。该方法通过重组现有计算之间的交互关系来实现适应，而非直接修改计算本身。在序列学习任务上的验证表明，可访问性适应可以在减少能力修改的同时保持任务性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经网络主要依靠修改参数来适应新环境，虽然存在模块化、条件计算等方法，但它们没有区分"计算能力"和"计算可访问性"作为独立的自适应变量，忽略了通过重组已有计算关系来实现适应这一可能性。

Method: 形式化了可访问性可塑性原则，提出基于关系（relationship-based）的操作实现，并建立"重用优先"的适应层次结构——可访问性修改优先于成本更高的能力和结构修改。

Result: 在序列学习任务上的概念验证评估表明，可访问性适应能够减少能力修改量，同时保持相当的任务性能。

Conclusion: 可访问性是一个独立的自适应维度，为未来计算关系可随环境演化的动态神经系统的研究提供了基础。

Abstract: Modern neural networks primarily adapt through parameter modification within predefined computational structures. While recent methods introduce modularity, conditional computation, and parameter-efficient adaptation, they generally do not distinguish computational capability from computational accessibility as separate adaptive variables. This work introduces Accessibility Plasticity, a principle of adaptive computation in which systems adapt not only by changing what computation exists, but also by reorganizing which existing computations can interact and participate. We formalize Accessibility Plasticity through a relationship-based operational realization and establish a reuse-first hierarchy of adaptation, where accessibility modification precedes more costly capability and structural changes. A proof-of-concept evaluation on sequential learning tasks shows that accessibility adaptation can reduce capability modification while maintaining comparable task performance. These results suggest accessibility as a distinct adaptive dimension and provide a foundation for future dynamic neural systems whose computational relationships evolve with changing environments.

</details>


### [55] [Hierarchical Grading in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22757)
*T. Shaska*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出分级大语言模型（GLLMs），一种为Transformer表示空间赋予分级的代数框架，通过几何不变量理论推导最优分级，证明其与标准Transformer在架构和推理复杂度上等价，并在分层目标下实现指数级风险分离。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Transformer缺乏对表示空间结构的代数约束，导致在分层或结构化目标上效率不足。作者希望引入分级代数结构，在保持Transformer表达能力与计算复杂度的前提下，提升其在层次化数据上的统计效率。

Method: 1) 在Transformer表示空间上定义分级结构，将加权标量作用传播至嵌入、自注意力和训练目标；2) 利用几何不变量理论中的Kempf-Ness泛函和Hilbert-Mumford判据，将最优分级刻画为开凸锥内的成员问题；3) 最优分级由两个矩映射的 coincidence point 给出闭式解；4) 标准Transformer是分级族边界上的半稳定各向同性点。

Result: 1) 对于分层目标，证明分级先验与无分级先验之间存在极小极大分离：在特定样本量窗口内，风险因子随层数指数衰减；2) 两个轮廓可离线估计，最优分级在训练前通过凸规划求解；3) 训练后分级被吸收进参数，每个GLLM可编译为相同架构和推理复杂度的标准Transformer。

Conclusion: GLLMs在不改变Transformer架构和推理成本的前提下，通过代数分级显著提升层次化数据的统计效率。标准Transformer只是该分级族中的一个边界特例而非最优解。该方法为预训练前的架构优化提供了理论指导。

Abstract: We introduce Graded Large Language Models (GLLMs), an algebraic framework that equips the representation space of a transformer with a grading and propagates the induced weighted scalar action through embeddings, self-attention, and the training objective. The construction extends the theory of graded neural networks and graded transformers to autoregressive language models while preserving expressive power, asymptotic computational complexity, and inference cost.
  The governing geometric picture is that of geometric invariant theory. The benefit of a grading is expressed by a Kempf--Ness functional on the grading torus; the grades that improve upon the uniform architecture form an open convex cone whose membership is decided by a Hilbert--Mumford-type criterion pairing a grade direction against two measurable profiles of the target and the data; the optimal grades are the coincidence point of two moment maps, given in closed form; and the ordinary transformer appears as a semistable isotropic point on the boundary of the cone: one member of a larger graded family rather than a distinguished optimum.
  Separately, for level-stratified targets we prove a minimax separation between the graded prior and its absence: over all estimators the risks of the graded and uniform target classes separate throughout an explicit window of sample sizes, by a factor that decays exponentially in the number of levels under geometric stratification. Both profiles are estimable offline, so the optimal grades solve a convex program certified before training begins. Because the grading is absorbed into the learned parameters after training, every GLLM compiles to a standard transformer of identical architecture and inference complexity.

</details>


### [56] [An Integrated Deep Learning and Statistical Framework for Whole-Network Gene--Environment Association with Leaf Vascular Architecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22763)
*Geran Zhao,Yangsheng Wang,Xiaotian Dai,Guifang Fu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出一个集成深度学习与统计的框架，用于从RGB图像中完整提取叶片脉管网络结构，并通过半参数稀疏典型相关分析（SSCCA）识别与叶片脉管结构相关的基因-环境交互作用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基因-环境关联研究仅使用少量低维摘要性状量化叶片脉管，丢弃了原始图像中大部分结构信息，需要一种能完整表征叶片脉管架构的方法。

Method: (1) 将叶片脉管架构表示为全网络图像表型；(2) 微调基于Transformer的边缘检测模型（EDTER）以联合学习局部和全局上下文特征，从RGB图像中提取完整脉管网；(3) 构建新的标注叶片图像数据库；(4) 应用半参数稀疏典型相关分析（SSCCA）进行变量选择和关联建模，通过截断潜高斯copula模型处理稀疏零膨胀的边缘图数据。

Result: 两个模拟实验验证了框架在不同复杂度下的性能；实际杨树数据集识别出三个与叶片脉管结构显著相关的基因-地理交互作用。

Conclusion: 该框架为高维复杂图像表型提供了广泛适用的方法论，为叶片脉管多样性的遗传基础提供了新见解。

Abstract: Leaf veins exhibit remarkable diversity in architecture and patterning, yet existing gene--environment association studies have primarily quantified leaf venation using a small collection of low-dimensional summary traits, thereby discarding most of the structural information contained in the original images. We propose an integrated deep learning and statistical framework. The proposed framework achieves four methodological advances. First, it represents the complete leaf vascular architecture as a whole-network image phenotype. Second, it fine-tunes the deep learning-based Edge Detection with Transformers (EDTER) model to accurately extract whole-network leaf vascular architecture from RGB images by jointly learning local and global contextual features. Third, it constructs a new annotated leaf image database by integrating edge maps generated by DiffusionEdge with the Berkeley Segmentation Database (BSDS500). Fourth, it applies Semiparametric Sparse Canonical Correlation Analysis (SSCCA) to perform variable selection and model associations between repeatedly measured high-dimensional Bivariate image responses and high-dimensional predictors while simultaneously accommodating sparse, zero-inflated data represented by edge maps through a truncated latent Gaussian copula model. Two simulation studies demonstrate the performance of the proposed framework under increasing levels of complexity. Application to a real \emph{Populus} dataset identifies three significant gene--geography interactions associated with leaf vascular architecture, providing new biological insights and establishing a broadly applicable methodological framework for high-dimensional complex image phenotypes.

</details>


### [57] [Beyond Shapley: An Influence-Based Data Auditing Pipeline for LLM Alignment and Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22766)
*Yunting Song,Matthew Watson,Peter Grabowski,Jun Qin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于Shapley值的可扩展、仅推理的数据估值流程，通过语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图并利用参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然变化来评估数据效用，从而识别梯度冲突记录。在HelpSteer2和HH-RLHF数据集上验证了该方法能够将人工审计搜索空间减少99.1%，并发现数千个隐藏的安全性和事实性偏好反转问题，揭示了现有基准评估中标注错误导致的严重漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模偏好数据和指令微调数据集中存在隐藏的结构性矛盾、安全风险和系统性人工标注错误，而标准的数据集审计方法（如语义去重或LLM-as-a-judge）难以捕捉单个记录对模型预测的实际影响，且常常遗漏深层功能规则冲突。

Method: 提出一种可扩展的、仅推理的数据估值流程：1) 近似Shapley值无需迭代模型重训练；2) 将语义k-NN邻域映射为有向图；3) 通过参考LLM的零样本和单样本条件对数似然变化直接评估数据效用；4) 将预测影响力分数转化为局部优势度量以隔离梯度冲突记录。

Result: 在HelpSteer2数据集上将人工审计搜索空间减少99.1%，成功发现多种失败模式下的错误标注记录；在Anthropic的HH-RLHF训练和评估分集中发现数千个隐藏的安全和事实偏好反转；揭示评估基准中严重漏洞，即高能力模型预测的安全/有用响应被有缺陷的人工标注惩罚。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一个数学基础扎实、高效的数据集诊断工具，能够发现人工标注错误、净化评估基准，并确保LLM对齐数据的完整性。

Abstract: The alignment of Large Language Models (LLMs) is increasingly bottlenecked by data quality. As datasets scale, massive preference and instruction-tuning corpora inevitably accumulate hidden structural contradictions, safety risks, and systemic human annotation errors. Standard dataset auditing methods, such as semantic deduplication or LLM-as-a-judge, struggle to capture the actual predictive impact of individual records and often miss deep functional rule clashes. To address this, we introduce a scalable, inference-only data valuation pipeline that approximates the Shapley value without iterative model retraining. By mapping semantic k-NN neighborhoods into a directed graph, our framework evaluates data utility directly through a reference LLM's probability distribution using zero-shot and one-shot conditional log-likelihood shifts. Our pipeline then translates these predictive influence scores into localized advantage metrics to isolate gradient-conflicting records. We demonstrate the pipeline's efficacy in sanitizing two heavily vetted alignment datasets. First, applying our pipeline to the HelpSteer2 dataset reduced the manual audit search space by 99.1%, successfully uncovering falsely-labeled records across diverse failure modes. Second, applying our automated audit strategy to Anthropic's HH-RLHF training and evaluation splits identified thousands of hidden safety and factual preference inversions. Crucially, by extending this audit to the evaluation split, we expose severe vulnerabilities in current benchmark integrity: highly capable models frequently predict the safer or more helpful response, only to be penalized by objectively flawed human ground-truth labels. Overall, our work provides a mathematically grounded, highly efficient diagnostic tool to uncover human label failures, sanitize evaluation benchmarks, and ensure the integrity of LLM alignment data.

</details>


### [58] [DomainPilot: Domain-Level Loss-Guided Two-Stage Data Mixture Optimization for Efficient Language Model Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22769)
*He Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: DomainPilot 提出了一种基于领域级损失引导的两阶段数据混合优化框架，通过在训练中监控领域损失信号，结合 Scaling Law 和 Mixing Law 分别进行粗优化和细优化，仅需约30行适配代码即可集成到现有训练框架中。在 Qwen3-1.7B 的 SFT 实验上，该框架在不增加数据量和训练成本的前提下，在多个基准上取得了显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态数据调度方法在工业级预训练和 SFT 中面临关键限制：数据选择在 TB 级语料上产生高昂的 O(N) 开销；混合优化方案存在严重 I/O 瓶颈或需要训练辅助参考模型；样本级重加权策略依赖的损失信号混淆了噪声、难度和新颖性。因此需要一种轻量级、高效的数据混合优化方法。

Method: DomainPilot 包含两阶段优化：（1）粗优化阶段，利用 Scaling Law 拟合领域级收敛曲线，推导混合调整的先验；（2）细优化阶段，通过受控扫掠实验建模跨领域交互效应，利用 Mixing Law 进一步优化混合比例。核心是 token 级领域损失监控机制，通过补丁式架构（约30行适配代码）注入现有训练框架，在不中断数据流水线的情况下捕获领域学习动态。

Result: 在 Qwen3-1.7B 的 SFT 上，优化后的数据混合相比原始混合在 MMLU-Redux 提升 +2%，AIME24 提升 +1.8%，LiveCodeBench v5 提升 +3.8%，BFCL v3 提升 +3.6%，且未增加总数据量或训练成本。

Conclusion: 领域级训练信号为数据混合优化提供了一种轻量级且有效的替代方案，避免了昂贵的数据选择或辅助模型训练。该方法验证了在无需复杂开销的前提下，通过粗-细两阶段优化即可显著提升模型性能。

Abstract: The training efficacy of large language models (LLMs) is fundamentally constrained by the quality and composition of training data. Existing dynamic data scheduling methods face critical limitations in industrial-scale pretraining and supervised fine-tuning (SFT): data selection incurs prohibitive O(N) costs on terabyte-scale corpora, mixture optimization schemes introduce severe I/O bottlenecks or require training auxiliary reference models, and sample-level reweighting strategies rely on loss signals that conflate noise, difficulty, and novelty.
  We present DomainPilot, a domain-level loss-guided two-stage data mixture optimization framework. DomainPilot introduces token-level domain loss monitoring to capture per-domain learning dynamics during training without halting the data pipeline. Building on these signals, we propose a Scaling Law guided coarse optimization stage that fits domain-specific convergence curves and derives a principled prior for mixture adjustment. A subsequent Mixing Law guided fine optimization stage refines the mixture by modeling cross-domain interaction effects through controlled sweep experiments. The entire mechanism is realized via a patch-based architecture that injects domain-aware loss computation into existing training frameworks (e.g., MindSpeed/Megatron-LM) with only ~30 lines of framework-specific adapter code.
  We validate DomainPilot on the Qwen3-1.7B model during SFT. Compared to the original data mixture, our optimized mixture achieves improvements of +2% on MMLU-Redux, +1.8% on AIME24, +3.8% on LiveCodeBench v5, and +3.6% on BFCL v3, without increasing total data volume or training cost. These results demonstrate that domain-level training signals provide an effective, lightweight alternative to expensive data selection or auxiliary model training for mixture optimization.

</details>


### [59] [Dementia Etiology Diagnosis via Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22770)
*Siyuan Du,Mengxi Chen,Xinyang Jiang,Zilong Wang,Jiangchao Yao,Dongsheng Li,Ya Zhang,Lili Qiu,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出COllaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement (COME)框架，通过将多中心采集语义、来源标识符和模态指示器作为异质性感知嵌入注入统一的Transformer架构，并结合信任区域约束优化，在七个独立队列上实现了痴呆症病因诊断的最新性能（平均宏观AUC 85.62%），比最强基线高出4.29个百分点，同时保持了优越的域外泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI在痴呆症病因诊断中因疾病间症状重叠而表现不佳；跨中心扩增数据虽能提升性能，但数据异质性导致冲突；传统多任务学习未考虑关键元信息（如中心特定采集信息和模态可用性）来应对异质性。

Method: (1) 将多中心采集语义、来源标识符和模态指示器作为异质性感知嵌入注入统一的Transformer架构；(2) 设计信任区域约束优化方案，通过参考模型在训练过程中正则化模型，防止伪相关。

Result: 在七个独立队列上，域内性能达到平均宏观AUC 85.62%，比最强基线提升4.29个百分点；在跨中心和跨序列评估中均保持优越的域外泛化能力；模型预测与已确立的生物标志物（淀粉样蛋白、tau）和临床严重程度一致。

Conclusion: COME框架能够实现鲁棒且可解释的痴呆症诊断，具备在真实世界场景中应用的潜力。

Abstract: Although artificial intelligence (AI) has shown promising performance in several medical tasks, accurate dementia etiology diagnosis with AI remains challenging due to complex overlapping symptoms among diseases. Scaling up the dataset size by combining the cross-center samples may bring a gain in the pursuit of performance, while the inherent data heterogeneity across centers or populations induces the conflict. Conventional multi-task learning paradigms offer a promising framework; however, they fail to consider critical meta information (e.g., site-specific acquisition and modality availability) to combat the heterogeneity. To address this challenge, we propose a Collaborative Meta Knowledge Enhancement (COME) framework for dementia etiology diagnosis, which injects multi-center acquisition semantics, source identifiers, and modality indicators as heterogeneity-aware embeddings into a unified Transformer architecture for scale-up training, enabling explicit modeling of heterogeneity. Besides, a trust-region constrained optimization scheme is designed to regularize the model from spurious correlations during training through a reference model. Across seven independent cohorts, our method achieves state-of-the-art in-domain performance with a mean macro-averaged AUC of 85.62% and a 4.29-point gain over the strongest baseline, while maintaining superior out-of-domain generalization under both cross-center and cross-sequence evaluations. Extensive validation also confirms the alignment between model predictions and established biomarkers (amyloid, tau) and clinical severity, highlighting the potential of COME to enable robust and interpretable dementia diagnostics in real-world settings.

</details>


### [60] [CC-AOS: Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Backward Induction for Finite-Horizon Optimal Stopping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22774)
*Tianwei Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CC-AOS方法，通过学习共享的延续价值模型（将状态、绝对时间、剩余时域和获取成本作为条件输入），实现连续成本和多时域下的在线最优停止决策，避免传统方法需要为每个运行点单独优化的低效问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数据驱动反向归纳方法需为每个成本-时域运行点单独求解，改变运行条件需重复优化并维护独立模型栈，无法高效支持连续成本适应和多时域部署。

Method: 基于联合摊销拟合反向归纳学习共享的延续价值模型，模型以当前状态、绝对时间、剩余时域和获取成本为条件；将价值函数和延续函数的单调性、凹性和时域依赖Lipschitz性质编码到模型架构中，并推导残差界限用于误差分析。

Result: 在受控高斯、时变非高斯过程及FordA发动机噪声基准上，CC-AOS在6个未见过的成本-时域组合中，单检查点比独立拟合的凸函数学习方法平均降低目标函数15.75%，且平均匹配调优的静态阈值。

Conclusion: CC-AOS通过结构化的摊销求解器实现了对连续成本和多时域最优停止问题的高效统一求解，泛化性能优越。

Abstract: Finite-horizon optimal stopping is a central problem in early time-series classification, where a system must decide at each sequence prefix whether the expected benefit of another observation justifies its acquisition cost. Existing data-driven backward-induction methods typically solve each cost-horizon operating point separately, so changing operating conditions requires repeated optimization and separate model stacks, making continuous cost adaptation and multi-horizon deployment inefficient. We propose CC-AOS (Cost- and Horizon-Conditioned Amortized Optimal Stopping), a structured amortized solver for a family of finite-horizon stopping problems with continuous costs and multiple horizons. CC-AOS learns a shared continuation-value model conditioned on the current state, absolute time, remaining horizon, and acquisition cost through joint amortized fitted backward induction. We establish that the exact value and continuation functions are nondecreasing, concave, and horizon-dependently Lipschitz in cost, encode these properties in the model architecture, and derive residual-based bounds on value and policy errors. Experiments on controlled Gaussian and time-varying non-Gaussian processes and the FordA engine-noise time-series benchmark compare CC-AOS with representative per-operating-point backward-induction solvers and tuned static stopping rules. At six unseen FordA cost-horizon pairs, one CC-AOS checkpoint achieved a lower terminal-risk-plus-sampling-cost objective than independently fitted Convex Function Learning at all six pairs, with an average reduction of 15.75 percent, while matching the tuned static thresholds on average.

</details>


### [61] [Predicting the Outcome of rTMS Depression Therapy using EEG Signals and CNN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22776)
*Wael Korani,Md Fahimul Kabir Chowdhury,Sadam AlQadi,Priyan Malarvizhi kumar,Reza Rostami,Reza Kazemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究利用FBSE-ED时频图像表示方法和轻量级CNN，在rTMS抑郁症治疗结果预测中达到93.60%的准确率，优于传统DWT方法和多种复杂深度学习模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重复经颅磁刺激(rTMS)是MDD的非侵入性疗法，但治疗反应存在个体差异，早期预测治疗结果有助于优化临床决策。

Method: 采用FBSE-ED和DWT两种时频方法将EEG信号转换为图像，设计轻量级定制CNN，使用10折交叉验证在私有rTMS数据库上训练，并在另一私有数据库上进行鲁棒性测试。

Result: FBSE-ED表示方法达到93.60%的最高分类准确率；相比DWT、EEG专用深度学习模型(提升3.62-10.72%)和预训练模型(提升23.03-27.35%)均有显著优势。

Conclusion: 将先进信号分解与深度学习结合可早期预测rTMS治疗反应，所提框架可解释性强、计算高效，适合在现实精神科诊所部署。

Abstract: Repetitive transcranial magnetic stimulation (rTMS) is a non invasive therapy for Major Depressive Disorder (MDD). In this study, we generate images using two time frequency methods to represent EEG signals: Fourier-Bessel Series Expansion with Euclidean Distance (FBSE-ED) and Discrete Wavelet Transform (DWT). We propose an efficient deep learning classifier to predict the outcome of rTMS depression therapy. In this study, we use a private rTMS databases to train a lightweight custom Convolutional Neural Network (CNN) using 10-fold cross validation strategy in order to avoid any bias in our results. The results show that the FBSE-ED representation achieves the highest classification accuracy of 93.60\%, outperforming traditional time-frequency technique (DWT). In addition, the proposed architecture with FBSE-ED image representation technique outperforms more complex EEG-Specific deep learning models (EEGNet, DeepConvNet, SleepEEGNet) by 3.62-10.72% and pretrained models (Xception, DenseNet201, and MobileNetV2) by 23.03-27.35%. For more experiments, we utilize another private rTMS database as test database to show the robustness of the proposed model. Our results suggest that integrating advanced signal decomposition with deep learning can facilitate early prediction of rTMS treatment response and support more targeted clinical decision-making. The proposed framework is interpretable, computationally efficient, and well-suited for deployment in real-world local psychiatric clinics.

</details>


### [62] [LC-SEPLM: long-range contact-supervised adaptation for sequence-only protein representation learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22777)
*Chen Wang,Boming Kang,Qinghua Cui*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LC-SEPLM 通过对 ESM2 进行长程残基对接触监督（使用 LoRA 微调）来引入三维结构信息，在仅需序列输入的推理中显著提升了蛋白质下游任务性能，尤其在远程同源识别任务上 Macro-F1 提升 6.47 个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有蛋白质语言模型（如 ESM2）虽能学习可迁移的序列表示，但其训练目标仅对氨基酸序列的上下文依赖关系建模，未显式约束模型学习蛋白质折叠后形成的三维残基接触信息，导致序列表示中缺乏空间结构知识。

Method: 在 ESM2 基础上引入 LoRA 进行参数高效微调，并添加长程残基对接触监督损失；使用成对特异性查询（pair-specific queries）通过交叉注意力机制在整个序列上提取与长程空间接触相关的全局序列上下文；在 50 万个 AlphaFold Swiss-Prot 蛋白质上训练。

Result: 在全部 8 个蛋白质级别下游任务上优于 ESM2；远程同源识别任务 Macro-F1 从 0.6122 提升至 0.6769（+0.0647）；在 ESM-S EC 基准测试上最大绝对增益达 0.1771。

Conclusion: 残基对接触监督是在保持仅序列推理的前提下，向蛋白质序列表示中引入结构信息的一种有界（bounded）有效途径。

Abstract: Protein language models learn transferable sequence representations. However, because they primarily model contextual dependencies along amino-acid sequences, their training objectives do not explicitly constrain the model to learn three-dimensional residue contacts formed after folding . Here, we introduce LC-SEPLM (Long-range Contact-supervised ESM Protein Language Model), which adapts ESM2 with LoRA and long-range residue-pair contact supervision while retaining sequence-only downstream inference. Pair-specific queries use cross-attention over the complete sequence to extract global sequence context associated with long-range spatial contacts. To expose the model to diverse structural information, we trained LC-SEPLM on 500,000 AlphaFold Swiss-Prot proteins. In downstream evaluation, LC-SEPLM improved all eight protein-level tasks relative to ESM2. The largest gain occurred in remote-homology recognition, where macro-F1 increased from 0.6122 to 0.6769 (+0.0647, or 6.47 percentage points). On the official ESM-S EC benchmark, LC-SEPLM also outperformed ESM-S with a maximum absolute gain of 0.1771. These results support residue-pair contact supervision as a bounded route for introducing structural information into protein sequence representations while preserving sequence-only inference.

</details>


### [63] [Multimodal Surface EMG Hand Gesture Recognition Using Query-Based Transformers for Prosthetic Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22779)
*Federico Del Pup,Elisa Tentori,Manfredo Atzori*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出EMG-CrossFormer，一种混合卷积-Transformer架构，用于融合多模态生理信号（sEMG、惯性、眼动数据）进行手势识别。该模型通过级联交叉注意力融合层整合多个单模态编码器的表征，并使用可学习手势查询解码。在四个NinaPro数据集上，仅用sEMG达到52-79%准确率，融合惯性信号后提升至80-93%，证明局部-全局特征联合建模与多模态融合的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度学习手势识别方法面临两个核心挑战：1) 模型性能随手势类别增加而下降（统计复杂度问题）；2) 主流方法依赖低延迟单模态卷积架构，卷积操作局限在局部窗口，无法捕获长程时序模式，且单模态设置无法利用惯性、眼动追踪等协调信号的互补信息。

Method: 提出EMG-CrossFormer，一种端到端混合卷积-Transformer架构。核心设计包括：1) 任意数量单模态编码器（可处理sEMG、惯性、眼动等不同生理序列）；2) 级联交叉注意力融合层，用于整合多模态表征；3) 可学习手势查询解码器，从融合表征中解码手势类别。

Result: 在四个NinaPro数据集（DB2, DB3, DB7, DB10）上评估，与六种SOTA模型对比。仅使用sEMG时平均准确率分别为72.33%, 52.48%, 79.16%, 73.49%；融合惯性信号后提升至90.66%, 80.40%, 92.79%, 92.06%。

Conclusion: 局部-全局特征联合建模能改善sEMG单独解码的性能，多模态融合进一步显著放大这一优势，证实了这两个设计原则对于复杂手势识别任务的价值。

Abstract: Hand gesture recognition via surface electromyography (sEMG) is fundamental to prosthetic control. In this field, deep learning approaches have become the gold standard. However, current architectures struggle to scale; model performance typically decreases as the number of hand movements increases. Performance degradation is tied to the increased statistical complexity of decoding expanded gesture sets and compounded by the limitations of state-of-the-art methods, which primarily rely on low-latency unimodal convolutional architectures. Convolutions operate locally, limiting model's ability to capture long-range sequential patterns. Unimodal setups cannot leverage complementary information from coordinated signals characterizing movement execution, such as inertial and eye-tracking data. These limitations motivate architectures that integrate local and global features across multimodal physiological sequences. To bridge this gap, this study introduces EMG-CrossFormer, an end-to-end hybrid convolutional-transformer for seamless multimodal integration. EMG-CrossFormer combines representations from an arbitrary number of unimodal encoders through cascaded cross-attention fusion layers, and decodes the fused representations using learnable gesture queries. EMG-CrossFormer was evaluated on four NinaPro datasets (DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10) and benchmarked against six state-of-the-art models using an increasing number of modalities. Using only sEMG, EMG-CrossFormer achieved mean accuracies of 72.33%, 52.48%, 79.16%, and 73.49% on DB2, DB3, DB7, and DB10, respectively. Incorporating inertial signals improved performance to 90.66%, 80.40%, 92.79%, and 92.06%. These results show that joint local-global feature modeling improves sEMG-only decoding and that multimodal fusion substantially amplifies this benefit, underscoring the value of both design principles for complex hand gesture recognition.

</details>


### [64] [What Softmax Throws Away: Mass-Aware Attention for Evidence Accumulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22781)
*Minwoo Yu,Young-guk Ha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出质量感知注意力（MAA），通过将标准L1归一化推广到Lp族，使重复输入下分子与分母以不同速率缩放，从而在表示幅度中保留有效输入数量信息。MAA不增加监督、参数或显式计数特征，在多个时序图模型和数据集上提升了任务性能与表示可恢复性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高任务性能无法保证模型内部表示保留了预测相关的结构性信息。时序图模型即使在未来链接预测上表现优异，其表示仍难以恢复基本的图统计量。作者发现这一差距源于标准注意力中加权平均的重复不变性：当证据模式重复时，分子分母同速增长，导致不同累积证据量的输入产生相同聚合结果。

Method: 提出Mass-Aware Attention (MAA)，将标准L1归一化推广到Lp族。在重复输入下，MAA使分子与分母以不同速率缩放，从而将有效输入数量信息保留在表示幅度中。该方法不增加监督信号、参数、隐藏维度或显式计数特征，且当p=1时退化为标准注意力。

Result: 在四个连续时序动态图模型和三个数据集上，MAA在11/12个模型-数据集组合中提升了未来链接AUC；线性可恢复性平均提升4.49%；优先连接恢复在12个组合中均有改善（经族系校正后）。在标记时序点过程、时序知识图谱、检索增强生成和时空点过程中也观察到一致的证据。

Conclusion: 信息可访问性与任务效用仍然存在区别：MTPP中NLL改善，TKG中排序基本保留，RAG中额外信息未改善诊断头，STPP中下游LayerNorm可能擦除信号。MAA作为一种通用归一化原则，通过控制标准注意力中的重复不变性来改善面向预测器的表示信息量。

Abstract: High task performance does not show whether a model retains prediction-relevant structural information in its internal representation. Temporal graph models, for example, can achieve high future-link AUC while basic graph statistics remain difficult to recover from the same representation. We identify one source of this gap in the weighted averaging used by standard attention: when an evidence pattern is repeated, the numerator and denominator grow at the same rate, so inputs with different amounts of accumulated evidence can produce the same aggregate.
  We propose Mass-Aware Attention (MAA), which generalizes standard L1 normalization to an Lp family. Under repetition, MAA makes the numerator and denominator scale at different rates, retaining the effective number of contributing inputs in the representation magnitude. It adds no supervision, parameters, hidden dimensions, or explicit count features, and recovers standard attention at p=1.
  Across four continuous-time dynamic graph models and three datasets, MAA improves future-link AUC in 11 of 12 model-dataset cells. Linear recovery from the same hidden representation increases by 4.49% on average, and preferential-attachment recovery improves in all 12 cells after family-wise correction. We also observe consistent evidence in marked temporal point processes, temporal knowledge graphs, retrieval-augmented generation, and spatio-temporal point processes. Information accessibility and task utility remain distinct: NLL improves in MTPP, ranking is largely preserved in TKG, additional information in RAG does not improve the diagnostic head, and downstream LayerNorm can erase the signal in STPP. These results position MAA as a general normalization principle for improving predictor-facing representation informativeness by controlling repetition invariance in standard attention.

</details>


### [65] [Optimizing Transformer Neural Network for Real-Time Outlier Detection on FPGAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22786)
*Ilia Sobakinskikh,Paul Alexander Bilokon*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文探索如何优化Transformer推理时间，将其应用于金融时间序列的实时异常检测，并利用FPGA加速实现低延迟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金融时间序列数据（如资产价格）常含有错误或异常值，且数据量快速增长，导致下游处理任务不稳定甚至有害，亟需更准确且更快速的数据清洗方法。

Method: 探索不同Transformer架构用于时间序列建模，并在PYNQ-Z2 FPGA板上高效实现，以最小化推理延迟。

Result: 展示了Transformer在时间序列异常检测中的应用效果，并验证了FPGA实现能够有效加速计算、降低延迟。

Conclusion: Transformer结合FPGA硬件加速能够高效实现金融时间序列的实时异常检测，兼顾精度与速度。

Abstract: In this work, we explore how the inference time of a Transformer Neural Network can be efficiently optimized with applications to real-time anomaly detection in financial time series. The financial time series are price series such as asset prices. Unfortunately, the data is often with errors or outliers that make the downstream data processing tasks useless, unstable or even harmful. Moreover, the amount of financial time-series data has been significantly increasing. Hence, there is a need for better data-cleaning methods in terms of accuracy and in terms of processing speed.
  Transformers as a neural network architecture have achieved superior performances in many tasks such as Natural Language Processing and Computer Vision. Time series modelling and especially anomaly detection tasks can benefit from the features of transformers architecture in multiple ways, including the capacity to capture long-range dependencies and interactions.
  Increasingly powerful hardware, such as field-programmable gate arrays (FPGAs), have seen increasing usage in recent years due to their reconfigurability and high performance. They can be efficiently utilized to speed up the computations of the Transformer architecture.
  We explore different Transformer architectures for time series modelling and how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board (PYNQ-Z2). In particular, we examine the application of Transformers to detect anomalies in time series and we show how they can be efficiently implemented on an FPGA board to minimize latency.
  The code is available at https://github.com/thxi/icl_thesis

</details>


### [66] [FMOPF: Latent Flow Matching with Constraint-Aware Interaction Priors for AC Optimal Power Flow](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22788)
*Zhilin Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出FMOPF框架，通过潜在流匹配解耦压缩与生成、约束感知交互先验网络显式建模负载-状态耦合，解决了现有生成模型在原始状态空间中生成可行近优解质量差且无法扩展到大规模系统的问题，在IEEE标准系统上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可再生能源渗透率加深，电力系统运营商需要可行近优解分布的完整刻画用于风险量化、灵敏度分析和多目标权衡评估，而传统监督神经网络仅能提供点预测，现有扩散生成模型在原始状态空间操作时解质量下降且无法扩展到百节点以上系统。

Method: 将高维OPF解流形压缩与条件映射生成两个任务解耦，采用潜在流匹配在隐空间进行生成；设计约束感知交互先验网络显式建模负载条件与状态变量之间的耦合关系。

Result: 在四个IEEE测试系统上，FMOPF提供最有效的Newton-Raphson热启动，在生成方法中实现最低尾部风险，且是首个能在数百节点系统上保持完全可行性的生成方法。消融实验证实潜生成管道是物理可行性的必要条件，交互先验起到尾部风险控制器作用。

Conclusion: 通过解耦压缩与生成任务并显式建模负载-状态耦合，FMOPF实现了大规模电力系统可行近优解的高质量生成，为高风险决策中的分布刻画提供了有效工具。

Abstract: AC optimal power flow determines the minimum-cost generation dispatch under nonlinear power balance constraints and is solved thousands of times daily in electricity market operations. Learning a direct mapping from load conditions to OPF solutions can accelerate this computation, yet with deepening renewable penetration, a single optimal dispatch is no longer sufficient. Operators require a characterization of the distribution of feasible near-optimal solutions for risk quantification, sensitivity analysis, and multi-objective trade-off assessment. Supervised neural networks provide fast point predictions but cannot capture this conditional distribution. Diffusion-based generative models can sample diverse solutions in principle, yet existing methods operating in the raw state space exhibit degraded solution quality and fail to scale beyond medium-sized systems. We identify the root cause as the conflation of two distinct tasks within a single model. Compressing the high-dimensional OPF solution manifold is one task, and learning the conditional mapping from loads to that manifold is another. This paper presents FMOPF, a framework that resolves this conflation by decoupling compression from generation through latent flow matching and by explicitly modeling load-state coupling through a Constraint-Aware Interaction Prior Network. Experiments on four IEEE test systems demonstrate that FMOPF provides the most effective Newton-Raphson warm starts, achieves the lowest tail risk among generative methods, and is the first such method to scale to systems with several hundred buses while preserving full feasibility. Ablation studies confirm that the latent generation pipeline is a necessary condition for physical feasibility and that the interaction prior functions as a late-stage tail-risk controller.

</details>


### [67] [Multimodal Domain Generalization for Depression Detection: An Attention-Based BiLSTM Network with Domain-Adversarial Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22794)
*Ali Tabaraei,Federico Simonetta,Stavros Ntalampiras*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出首个基于域泛化的患者独立多模态抑郁检测框架，通过BiLSTM结合注意力机制与梯度反转层，在Androids-Corpus数据集上达到93.2%准确率和94.2% F1分数，超越现有基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于深度学习的自动抑郁检测因说话者间差异导致的域偏移问题，泛化能力受限；本文旨在解决这一关键问题，实现患者独立的鲁棒抑郁检测。

Method: 提出多模态域泛化框架，集成双向长短期记忆网络（BiLSTM）与模态内及跨模态注意力机制，采用片段级融合进行决策；引入基于域对抗训练神经网络（DANN）的梯度反转层，通过对抗性限制模型识别个体说话者的能力，促进域不变表征，减少患者特定偏差。

Result: 在Androids-Corpus数据集上采用5折交叉验证，确定MelSpec和ItalianBERT为最优基线组合（30秒片段时长）；加入域泛化后准确率提升2.5%，F1分数提升3.3%，最终达到93.2%准确率、93.2%精确率、96.2%召回率、94.2% F1分数，超越所有现有基准。

Conclusion: 消融实验验证了多模态融合、深度架构选择和域泛化的各自贡献，表明三者结合对实现鲁棒且可泛化的抑郁检测至关重要。

Abstract: Automatic depression detection with deep learning has shown promise but often suffers from limited generalization due to domain shift arising from inter-speaker variability. To address this critical issue, we present the first patient-independent multimodal depression detection framework that incorporates domain generalization (DG), jointly leveraging both acoustic and textual modalities. The proposed model integrates bidirectional Long Short-Term Memory (BiLSTM) with intra- and cross-modal attention mechanisms, accompanied by segment-level fusion for decision-making. Generalization is further enhanced by applying a gradient reversal layer inspired by Domain-Adversarial Training of Neural Networks (DANN), which promotes domain-invariant representations by adversarially limiting the model's ability to identify individual speakers, effectively reducing patient-specific bias. Conducting experiments on the Androids-Corpus dataset with a 5-fold cross-validation (CV) protocol, various pairings of audio and text feature extractors were evaluated over different segment durations, determining MelSpec and ItalianBERT as the optimal baseline at a 30-second segment duration. The addition of DG to this baseline yields a 2.5% increase in accuracy and 3.3% in F1-score, achieving 93.2% accuracy, 93.2% precision, 96.2% recall, and 94.2% F1-score, surpassing all existing benchmarks. Extensive ablation studies assess the impact of multimodal fusion, deep architectural choices, and DG, highlighting their combined contribution to robust and generalizable depression detection.

</details>

### [68] [Structured Coordination Games on Planar Graphs: a Dual Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22865)
*John S. McAlister*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究结构化协调博弈中的纳什均衡，发现其与图的最小割划分存在局部对应关系，并通过平面偶图建立了拟度量空间，将纳什均衡定义为全局目标的局部最小化器。在双策略限制下，构建了动态子图过程来复现近视最优响应动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统纯协调博弈在完全连接图中纳什均衡求解是平凡的，但当玩家之间存在关系结构（即结构化图）时，问题变得复杂。本文旨在揭示结构化协调博弈中纳什均衡与图划分之间的深层联系。

Method: 通过势函数建立纳什均衡与最小割划分的局部对应关系；利用平面偶图中划分与子图的标准关系定义拟度量空间；在双策略限制下构建动态子图过程。

Result: 证明了结构化协调博弈的纳什均衡对应最小割划分的局部概念；在拟度量空间中纳什均衡是全局目标的局部最小化器；构建的动态子图过程能够复现近视最优响应动力学。

Conclusion: 本文为结构化协调博弈的纳什均衡提供了图论视角下的新理解，通过最小割与拟度量空间建立了理论基础，并给出了与经典动力学过程的联系。

Abstract: Finding Nash equilibria in the pure coordination game is trivial when every player interacts with every other player evenly. However, when players have a relational structure, the question becomes harder to answer. Here, we show that Nash equilibria to the structured coordination game represent a local notion of the Min-cut partition by way of the potential function. Using the standard relationship between partitions and subgraphs in the planar dual, we define a quasimetric space for which Nash equilibria are local minimizers of a global objective. When the game is restricted to only two strategies, these results allow us to construct a dynamic subgraph process which recapitulates the Myopic Best Response dynamics of the coordination game.

</details>


### [69] [On the Power of Deception in Repeated Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23049)
*Saba Ahmadi,Avrim Blum,Dimitar Chakarov,Melissa Dutz*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究重复博弈中针对基于计数的对手学习者的欺骗策略，形式化了欺骗与非欺骗行为，定义了"欺骗红利"，设计了精确动态规划与近似算法，并证明了近似最优欺骗收益的NP-hard性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在重复博弈中，对手通过观察历史行为预测我方下一步行动，这为欺骗提供了可能——我方可以有意在一段时间内以某种方式行动以塑造对手预期，然后切换策略利用对手的反应获利。然而，如何系统性地研究这种欺骗行为及其收益边界尚不明确。

Method: 形式化定义了欺骗与非欺骗行为及"欺骗红利"（最优欺骗策略超出最优固定混合策略的收益增益）；对一般和博弈建立欺骗的结构性结果；针对小动作空间或小记忆长度设计精确动态规划；针对大记忆或长时间跨度设计近似算法；提供学习对手未知计数学习规则的近似算法；证明针对ERM学习规则的最优欺骗收益近似问题是NP-hard的（包括常数因子近似和T^α加法近似）；在随机博弈上进行经验测量。

Result: 建立了欺骗的结构性理论基础；提出了精确和近似的算法框架；证明了针对ERM学习规则的最优欺骗收益近似是NP-hard的；实验测量了随机博弈中的欺骗红利。

Conclusion: 针对基于计数的对手学习者，欺骗可以在博弈中获得额外收益，但最优欺骗策略的计算在一般情况下是困难的，需要根据问题规模选择精确或近似算法。

Abstract: In repeated games, opponents often predict what we'll do next by looking at what we have done so far. This allows us to deceive them: we can deliberately behave one way for a period of time to shape their expectations, then switch strategies to profit from the induced response. We study deception in repeated two-player normal-form games against count-based learners, whose behavior depends only on how often we have played each action in the past. We formalize deceptive and non-deceptive play, and introduce the notion of a deception bonus, the payoff gain of the best deceptive strategy over the best fixed mixed strategy. We establish structural results on deception in general-sum games. We design exact dynamic programs for optimizing against any count-based learner when the action space or opponent's memory is small, and develop approximation algorithms for settings where the opponent's memory or the time horizon is large. We also provide an approximation algorithm for learning to deceive an opponent whose count-based learning rule is unknown. To complement our algorithmic results, we show that approximating the optimal deceptive payoff against the classic Empirical Risk Minimization (ERM) learning rule is NP-hard, including obtaining any constant-factor approximation or even a $T^α$-additive approximation for any $0 < α< 1$. Finally, we empirically measure the deception bonus in random games with i.i.d. payoffs.

</details>


### [70] [Shapley Meets Tutte](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23106)
*Martin Loebl*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究存在预对齐代理群体的合作博弈，每个预对齐群体大小为2（如道路网络中的路段、数据集中的属性对）。目标是计算每个局部连接（预对齐对）对网络连通性的贡献，使用Shapley值建模，并将其与色多项式、Tutte多项式以及统计物理中Potts模型的配分函数联系起来。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在网络存在对抗攻击或需要防御规划的场景下，需要量化每个局部连接对整体网络连通性的贡献，以便分配利润或评估关键性。

Method: 将网络连通性增强的局部值（由局部连接失效概率等特征函数决定）的Shapley值与色多项式、Tutte多项式以及Potts模型的配分函数建立联系。

Result: 建立了Shapley值与色多项式、Tutte多项式及Potts模型配分函数之间的理论关联。

Conclusion: 这种联系使得可以利用图论和统计物理中的成熟工具来计算和分析合作博弈中的贡献分配问题。

Abstract: We initiate the study of cooperative games where there exist groups of pre-aligned agent. For example, in a road network, the agents are cross-roads and pre-aligned groups are road-segments. For a set of datasets, the agents are attributes and each database which connects two (or more) attributes is pre-aligned. In this paper, each pre-aligned group has size two.
  Our goal is to find a way to determine the contribution of each individual local connection (pre-aligned couple) to the connectivity of the network, having an adversarial attack in mind or planning a defense of the network, or wanting to split the profits of the network between various owners of the individual local connections of the network. We model these contributions by Shapley values of the connectivity augmented 'local values' which are characteristic functions determined, e.g., by failure probabilities of local connections.
  We link the concepts of potential and Shapley values of these connectivity augmented local values to the world of chromatic and Tutte polynomials and the partition function of the Potts model from statistical physics.

</details>


### [71] [Network Allocation Games with Anonymous Preferences](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23248)
*Argyrios Deligkas,Eduard Eiben,Šimon Schierreich,Alexandros A. Voudouris*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 研究匿名战略主体在图形拓扑顶点上的网络分配博弈，探讨基于交换和跳跃的稳定性概念的存在性与计算问题，给出了关于效用函数结构和拓扑的二分法，并证明了福利最大化分配和帕累托最优验证的强难解性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究网络分配博弈中匿名战略主体在不同稳定性概念下的稳定分配的存在性与计算问题，填补该领域在交换和跳跃稳定性方面的理论空白。

Method: 通过分析基于交换（agents交换位置）和基于跳跃（agents跳转到空顶点）的稳定性概念，结合效用函数结构和图拓扑结构，对稳定分配的存在性及计算复杂性进行分类研究。

Result: 针对大多数稳定性概念，给出了存在性与难解性之间的二分法，该二分法依赖于效用函数结构和图拓扑；同时证明了计算福利最大化分配和验证帕累托最优分配是强难解问题。

Conclusion: 网络分配博弈中稳定分配的存在性和计算复杂性高度依赖于效用函数结构和图拓扑，且福利优化和帕累托最优验证问题本质上是困难的。

Abstract: We study network allocation games in which a set of agents must be allocated to (a subset of) the vertices of a graph topology. The agents are anonymous and strategic: Each of them aims to maximize her utility, which is a function of the number of agents in the neighborhood of their allocated vertex. We focus on the existence and the computation of stable allocations under various notions of stability. More specifically, we study swap-based stability notions, where agents can exchange locations between them, and jump-based stability notions, where agents can jump to an empty vertex of the graph. For almost all stability notions we consider, we provide dichotomies between existence and intractability that depend on the structure of the utility functions of the agents and the topology. In addition, we prove strong intractability results for computing welfare-maximizing allocations and for verifying whether a given allocation is Pareto optimal.

</details>


### [72] [Online Fair Division with Budget Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23310)
*Saar Cohen,Nicholas Teh,Paul W. Goldberg,Michael J. Wooldridge*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究在线离散公平分配问题，在广义分配预算约束下，物品逐一到达需不可撤销地分配给可行智能体或慈善机构，公平性仅基于每个接收者预算可行子集评估。研究发现无额外结构时确定性在线算法无法保证近似无嫉妒性，但提出有界密度扩散条件可恢复有意义的保证，并进一步研究了资源增强和学习增强框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将公平分配问题拓展到在线场景并引入广义分配预算约束，探索在资源有限、信息不完全的动态环境中如何实现公平分配。

Method: 首先证明无额外结构时确定性在线算法无法保证近似无嫉妒性；然后引入有界密度扩散条件，为任意物品大小设计近似算法；研究资源增强（允许算法使用略大于基准的预算）的效果；最后开发基于预测联合价值-大小类型的学习增强框架。

Result: 无额外结构时无法保证近似公平；有界密度扩散条件可恢复有意义的公平保证；在共同估价和小物品条件下可达到最优确定性前沿；资源增强可改善可实现的保证；学习增强框架在完美预测下具有一致性，对预测误差具有鲁棒性，但单独预测价值和大小边际无法恢复强公平保证。

Conclusion: 在线公平分配在广义预算约束下需要结构条件（如有界密度扩散）才能实现有意义的公平保证，资源增强和学习增强是有效的改进策略。

Abstract: We study an online variant of discrete fair division under generalized assignment budget constraints. Goods arrive one at a time and must be assigned irrevocably to a feasible agent or to charity, which holds all unallocated goods, while fairness is evaluated only against budget-feasible subsets of every recipient's bundle. We first show that, without additional structure, no deterministic online algorithm can guarantee any fixed approximation to feasible envy-freeness, even in highly symmetric instances. We then identify bounded density spread as a structural condition that restores meaningful guarantees, obtaining approximation algorithms for arbitrary item sizes and showing that, under common valuations and sufficiently small goods, these guarantees can be strengthened to an optimal deterministic frontier. We further study resource augmentation, where the online algorithm is allowed slightly larger budgets than the fairness benchmark, and characterize the resulting improvement in the achievable guarantees. Finally, we develop a learning-augmented framework based on predicting joint value-size types, proving consistency under perfect predictions, robustness to prediction error, and showing that separate predictions of value and size marginals are insufficient to recover strong fairness guarantees.

</details>


### [73] [Fair Division with Strictly Increasing Valuations: A Tight Threshold for Two-Agent EF1 and PO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23367)
*Nicholas Teh*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究不可分割商品分配中，严格正边际价值能否恢复EF1（至多一件商品羡慕自由）与PO（帕累托最优）的兼容性。对于两个智能体，确定了商品数量的精确阈值：当商品数≤7时，在严格递增估值下总存在EF1+PO分配（无需子模性假设）；当商品数=8时，存在反例（即使满足归一化、整数、严格递增、子模估值）。此外，将三智能体的NP-hardness结果强化：即使零边际值限制在涉及单个智能体的8个固定智能体-商品对上，问题仍是NP-hard。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究严格正边际价值（strictly positive marginal values）能否在不可分割商品分配中恢复EF1与PO的兼容性，并确定两智能体场景下兼容性成立的确切商品数量阈值。

Method: 理论分析：证明两智能体在≤7个商品时EF1+PO分配的存在性；构造8商品反例证明阈值紧性；通过归约方法强化三智能体问题的NP-hardness结论。

Result: 1) 两智能体且商品数≤7时，严格递增估值下总存在EF1+PO分配；2) 8商品时存在归一化、整数、严格递增、子模估值的反例，其中所有EF1分配均被严格帕累托支配；3) 三智能体问题在严格限制零边际值条件下仍是NP-hard。

Conclusion: 两智能体场景下，严格正边际价值并非总能保证EF1与PO兼容，兼容性依赖于商品数量（≤7可行，≥8不可行）；三智能体场景下，即使大幅限制零边际值出现的位置，判定EF1+PO分配是否存在仍是NP-hard。

Abstract: We study whether strictly positive marginal values restore the compatibility of envy-freeness up to one good (EF1) and Pareto optimality (PO) for indivisible goods. For two agents, we identify the exact threshold in the number of goods. Every instance with at most seven goods and strictly increasing valuations admits an allocation that is both EF1 and PO, without any submodularity assumption. In contrast, we construct an eight-good instance with normalized, integer-valued, strictly increasing, submodular valuations in which every EF1 allocation is strictly Pareto dominated. Thus, eight goods are necessary and sufficient for a two-agent counterexample. Finally, we strengthen the three-agent NP-hardness result of Chandramouleeswaran and Nimbhorkar (2026): deciding whether an EF1 and PO allocation exists remains NP-hard for normalized, integer-valued, monotone submodular valuations even when zero marginals are confined to eight fixed agent-good pairs, all involving a single agent.

</details>


### [74] [The Tiered Clinching Auction with Applications to Carbon Offset Markets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23874)
*Tessa Davis,Agnès Totschnig,Adrian Vetta*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于碳抵消市场的新型上升拍卖机制，该机制将Ausubel的抓拍拍卖推广到具有层级质量物品的场景中。该机制在多个嵌套子市场中实现抓拍分配，在竞拍者对质量有最低接受阈值且边际效用递减的假设下，能够产生有效结果并收取VCG价格，诚实出价是弱占优策略，且可在多项式时间内实现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 碳抵消市场存在流动性低的问题：抵消额度高度异质、认证体系碎片化、缺乏集中交易基础设施导致价格信号不清晰。需要设计一种能够处理质量层级差异的拍卖机制来改善市场效率。

Method: 将Ausubel抓拍拍卖推广到层级质量物品场景。核心思想：当对手需求低于供给时，竞拍者获得物品分配。通过构建多个嵌套子市场，在层级质量情形下实现抓拍。假设竞拍者有最低可接受质量门槛、对超出部分质量无差异且边际效用递减。

Result: 拍卖产生有效分配结果，收取VCG价格；诚实出价是弱占优策略（在私有价值假设下）；算法可在多项式时间内实现。

Conclusion: 该机制不仅适用于碳抵消市场，还可推广至任何物品可按质量排序且竞拍者存在质量截止点的市场场景，如酒店房间预订、音乐会票务和赞助搜索拍卖等。

Abstract: Voluntary carbon offsetting is a strategy which has been pursued globally by corporations to reduce their effective carbon emissions. Carbon offset markets currently suffer from low liquidity: offsets are highly heterogeneous, the landscape of accreditation is fragmented, and the market lacks a centralized trading infrastructure which would provide clear demand and price signaling to producers and buyers of offsets. In this paper, we propose an ascending auction mechanism which can be applied to the sale of carbon offsets. It generalizes Ausubel's clinching auction to a setting with items of tiered quality. The central idea behind the clinching mechanism is that bidders are allocated items when their opponents' demand drops below the supply. This is generalized to multiple nested submarkets where clinching can occur in the tiered case. Assuming that bidders have a minimum quality level that they will accept but are indifferent to quality beyond that, along with having decreasing marginal utility, we obtain that the auction generates the efficient outcome and charges VCG prices. As a result, sincere bidding is a weakly dominant strategy given private values. Moreover, the auction can be implemented in polynomial time. Beyond offset markets, this auction can be applied to any market where items can be ordered on a scale and bidders have a cutoff point for desiring items on the scale, such as hotel room bookings, concert ticketing and sponsored search auctions.

</details>


### [75] [Algorithms for Equilibria in Concurrent Stopping Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24219)
*Léonard Brice,Thomas A. Henzinger,K. S. Thejaswini*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文研究并发博弈中带约束的纳什均衡存在性问题（该问题不可判定），通过两种途径实现可处理性：一是引入ε-近似纳什均衡的概念，提出指数时间算法并证明PSPACE-hard下界；二是采用极端风险敏感均衡（XRSE）概念，证明其约束存在性问题是NP完全的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 并发博弈中带约束的纳什均衡存在性问题是不可判定的，即使对于10人终止博弈（几乎必然终止）也是如此。因此需要寻找可判定的近似或替代方案。

Method: 1) 松弛精确性要求，引入参数化的ε-近似纳什均衡（ε-NE），设计指数时间算法（复杂度关于ε位长呈多项式）；2) 松弛解概念，采用极端风险敏感均衡（XRSE），将玩家分为乐观者和悲观者，分别以最好/最差可达收益而非期望收益评估策略。

Result: 1) ε-NE近似约束存在性问题的算法运行时间指数级，关于ε位长多项式级，且该问题对回合制博弈和纯均衡已是PSPACE-hard；2) XRSE的约束存在性问题是NP完全的，与回合制博弈相同。

Conclusion: 通过松弛精确性（ε-NE）或松弛解概念（XRSE），可以将原本不可判定的约束存在性问题转化为可判定的复杂度类（指数时间、PSPACE-hard、NP-complete），为并发博弈的均衡计算提供了可行的研究路径。

Abstract: Concurrent games are a standard model for multi-agent systems, with Nash equilibrium as their central solution concept. The associated \emph{constrained existence problem}---does a game admit a Nash equilibrium whose expected payoff lies within a prescribed interval for every player?---is undecidable, and remains so even for 10-player \emph{stopping} games, in which a terminal state is reached almost surely under every strategy profile. We give two routes to tractability.
  We first relax exactness and consider the problem of approximate constrained existence problem, parametrised by $\varepsilon$-NE, which decides whether an \(\varepsilon\)-Nash equilibrium with the prescribed payoffs exists. The algorithm runs in exponential time, and only polynomially in the bit-size of \(\varepsilon\). We complement it with a \PSPACE-hardness lower bound that holds already for turn-based games, and for pure equilibria as well.
  We then relax the solution concept, turning to \emph{extreme risk-sensitive equilibria} (XRSE), recently introduced for turn-based stochastic games. Here the players are partitioned into optimists and pessimists, who evaluate a strategy profile by the best, respectively the worst, payoff attainable with positive probability, instead of the expected payoff. We prove that the constrained existence problem for XRSE is \NP-complete on concurrent games, as for turn-based games.

</details>


### [76] [CAP-DO: Learned Contextual Action Proposals for Certified Double-Oracle Solving Across Related Zero-Sum Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24610)
*Mu Wang,Zhenkun Liu,Liang Liang,Guofu Zhang*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文提出CAP-DO框架，通过离线训练的排序器为重复出现的上下文博弈生成初始动作集，从而热启动双Oracle算法，在保持完全博弈认证保证的同时，显著减少最佳响应调用次数，提升认证率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在安全与检查规划等系列零和博弈问题中，每个上下文（如目标价值、检查成本等变化）产生不同的收益矩阵。标准Double Oracle算法需要为每个新上下文从头开始搜索，重复发现上下文相关的动作，计算效率低下。

Method: CAP-DO分为离线训练和在线推理两阶段。离线阶段，从已求解的上下文中训练防守方和攻击方各自的排序器。在线阶段，固定排序器为每个新上下文提议初始受限动作集，随后通过完整的全空间最佳响应检查与双端认证证书决定是否接受输出。学习决定搜索起点，当前博弈通过最佳响应检查和证书机制保证输出质量。

Result: 在非加性上下文检查博弈基准测试中（每个玩家最多9,880个动作），CAP-DO-balanced相比冷启动、轨迹复用和启发式热启动方法，在固定扩展预算下实现了更高的认证率，并使用了更少的全空间最佳响应调用。

Conclusion: CAP-DO保留了DO的完全博弈认证保证（每个接受输出均满足预设证书容差），在标准精确Oracle假设下，当扩展无上限时仍保持有限收敛性。该框架有效结合了学习的效率与形式化保证的可靠性。

Abstract: Many security and inspection-planning problems require solving a sequence of related zero-sum games. Across this sequence, the feasible defender and attacker action spaces re-main fixed, whereas each context induces a different payoff matrix through changes in target values, inspection effective-ness, costs, and interaction effects. Double Oracle (DO) solves large zero-sum games without materializing the full payoff matrix by iteratively expanding a restricted game. However, applying standard DO independently to each new payoff context requires restarting the search from a generic restricted game and requires rediscovering context-relevant actions through full-space best responses. We propose Con-textual Action Proposal Double Oracle (CAP-DO), a learning-augmented framework that warm-starts DO for repeated contextual games. Offline, CAP-DO trains separate defender and attacker rankers once from solved contexts. Online, the fixed rankers propose initial restricted action sets for each new context. Learning therefore determines where certified search starts, while the current game, through full-space best-response checks and a two-sided certificate, still determines whether the output is accepted. Theoretically, CAP-DO pre-serves DO's full-game certification guarantee, so every accepted output meets the prescribed certificate tolerance. Under standard exact-oracle assumptions, CAP-DO also retains finite convergence when expansion is uncapped. Empirically, across three scales of a non-additive contextual inspection-game benchmark, with up to 9,880 actions per player, CAP-DO-balanced achieves higher certification rates and uses few-er full-space best-response calls than cold-start, trace-reuse, and heuristic warm starts under fixed expansion budgets.

</details>


### [77] [The Degree of Strategy-Proofness for Risk-Averse Committee Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24676)
*Dael Sinay,Rica Gonen*

Main category: cs.GT

TL;DR: 本文首次将风险规避真实性(RAT)程度框架扩展到基于审批的委员会(ABC)选举领域，聚焦比例批准投票(PAV)规则，揭示了其在子集操纵下的安全操纵边界：当操纵者知晓至少⌈n/k⌉个其他选民的选票时存在安全操纵可能，而当知晓不超过⌊n/(k+1)⌋-1个选民时则完全免疫。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典策略证明性假设操纵者要么完全了解他人投票，要么愿意承担风险，这与现实选民面临的不确定性不符。为填补RAT程度框架在多人赢家选举（尤其是ABC选举）中的研究空白，探索比例代表制与策略鲁棒性之间的张力。

Method: 将RAT程度框架扩展至ABC选举环境，以PAV规则为研究对象，分析其超集风险规避策略证明性（在候选人剔除场景下），推导安全操纵所需已知选票数量的紧界。

Result: 建立了PAV规则下安全操纵的紧界：当操纵者知晓f=⌈n/k⌉个其他选民选票时存在安全操纵；当知晓最多f=⌊n/(k+1)⌋-1个选民时完全免疫。

Conclusion: PAV规则在ABC选举中并非完全策略鲁棒，其安全操纵边界依赖于已知选票数量与委员会规模、选民总数的关系，为理解多赢家投票机制的策略脆弱性提供了量化视角。

Abstract: The classic notion of strategyproofness implicitly assumes that a manipulating agent either possesses complete knowledge of what all other agents are going to report, or is willing to take the risk and act as if they know these reports. To capture the profound uncertainty of real-world voters, recent work introduced \emph{risk-avoiding truthfulness (RAT)} and the \emph{RAT-degree}, which quantifies the exact number of known reports required for a manipulation to be strictly safe. While the RAT-degree has been analyzed in settings such as single-winner elections, its implications for multi-winner voting remain unexplored. In this paper, we bridge this gap by extending the RAT-degree framework to approval-based committee (ABC) selection, a domain characterized by a fundamental tension between proportional representation and strategic robustness. Focusing on the prominent Proportional Approval Voting (PAV) rule, we investigate its susceptibility to safe subset manipulations. We establish tight bounds on its superset risk-avoiding strategy-proofness under dropping candidates, demonstrating that PAV is vulnerable to safe manipulation when the agent knows the exact ballots of $f = \lceil n/k \rceil$ other voters, but remains completely immune given knowledge of at most $f = \lfloor \frac{n}{k+1} \rfloor - 1$ voters.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

### [78] [Concept-based Visual Counterfactual Explanations with Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22544)
*Yassine Oueslati,Daniil Kirilenko,Martin Gjoreski,Marc Langheinrich*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出C-VCE，一种将分类器直接嵌入扩散生成模型的概念瓶颈层中的视觉反事实解释框架，通过人类可解释的概念特征引导生成，无需依赖外部噪声鲁棒分类器，在保持图像质量的同时实现精准的预测翻转。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散的反事实解释方法依赖外部分类器，但外部分类器必须在噪声图像上可靠工作，这使其脆弱且难以部署用于鲁棒的解释。

Method: 将分类器直接构建到生成模型内部的概念瓶颈层中，通过人类可解释的概念特征引导反事实生成；添加概率正则化项平衡预测翻转与原始图像保持；使用基于梯度的掩码限制修改区域。

Result: 在CelebA等基准上，C-VCE匹配或提高了翻转率，同时生成的反事实图像在视觉上更接近输入、失真更小，优于依赖外部噪声分类器的基线方法。

Conclusion: 暴露和控制内部概念层是使强大生成模型更易理解、更安全使用的有前景的方法，C-VCE可作为视觉系统中需要具体反事实图像而不必依赖额外噪声鲁棒分类器的实用工具。

Abstract: Visual counterfactual explanations aim to answer "what minimal change to this image would flip the model's prediction?", and are increasingly important as vision models are deployed in safety-critical domains (e.g., medicine). Existing diffusion-based methods can produce realistic edits, but they rely on external classifiers that must work reliably on noisy images, which makes them fragile and hard to deploy for robust explanations.
