We introduce C-VCE, a new diffusion framework that builds the classifier directly into the generative model via a concept bottleneck layer, so that counterfactuals are guided by human-interpretable features (concepts) instead of a separate noise robust classifier that works with pixel-level edits. Our model lets users to toggle on/off semantic concepts during sampling, then minimally adjusts relevant image regions, while preserving the rest of the image, respecting feature correlations. To keep edits small and controlled, we add a simple probabilistic regularizer that balances "change the prediction" against "stay close to the original", plus a gradient-based mask that confines modifications to the most relevant regions. On benchmarks such as CelebA, C-VCE matches or improves flip rates while producing counterfactuals that are visually closer to the input and less distorted than baselines that depend on separate noisy-image classifiers. These properties make C-VCE a practical tool for vision systems where users need concrete "what-if" images without having to trust an additional, noise-robust classifier. More broadly, our results suggest that exposing and controlling an internal concept layer is a promising way to make powerful generative models easier to understand and safer to use.

</details>


### [79] [SeT-Diff: Towards Semantic Foundation Models for HPC Telemetry and Time-Series](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22548)
*Giovanni B. Esposito,Francesco Antici,Daniele Cesarini,Andrea Bartolini*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SeT-Diff是首个基于扩散模型的计算节点遥测时序基础模型，通过语义条件生成解耦系统动态与数据集结构，在真实超算数据集上实现重建MAE 0.047，并支持零样本置换稳定性、数据插补、预测及虚拟传感等多种任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有HPC遥测机器学习方法依赖固定位置传感器变量的静态子集，模型在目标任务变化或传感器指标变动时失效，缺乏灵活性和泛化能力。

Method: 提出SeT-Diff扩散模型，以每个传感器的语义描述为条件指导生成过程，将系统动态与数据集结构解耦，从而实现任务无关的灵活建模。

Result: 在真实超算数据集上重建MAE为0.047；支持零样本置换稳定性（传感器打乱后精度几乎无下降）；单一预训练模型可实现数据插补、预测和虚拟传感，热推断MAE达0.033。

Conclusion: SeT-Diff作为首个面向计算节点遥测的扩散基础模型，凭借语义条件化生成架构有效解耦系统动态与传感器结构，是实现HPC系统数据驱动数字孪生的有效方案。

Abstract: Data centers and their compute nodes require accurate and flexible digital twins capable of modeling the complex interplay of workloads, environmental parameters, and physical metrics. Current machine learning approaches for HPC and its telemetry typically rely on a static subset of anonymous, fixed-position sensor variables tailored to single tasks. Consequently, these models become obsolete when target tasks change or sensor metrics vary. We propose SeT-Diff, the first foundational model for compute node telemetry and time-series. Unlike rigid architectures, our diffusion-based approach conditions the generative process on each sensor's semantic description, decoupling the system dynamics from the structure of the dataset. Experiments on a real-world supercomputer dataset demonstrate a Mean Absolute Error (MAE) of 0.0470 on reconstruction tasks. SeT-Diff exhibits zero-shot permutation stability, maintaining accuracy with negligible degradation even when sensors are shuffled. A single pre-trained model effectively performs data imputation, forecasting, and virtual sensing - achieving a 0.033 MAE in thermal inference - making SeT-Diff an effective data-driven digital twin for HPC systems.

</details>


### [80] [QFoldAgent: An Autonomous Quantum Optimization Multi-Agent System for Protein Structure Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22549)
*Winson Chen,Yuqi Zhang,Sixu Chen,Nuo Xu,Qiang Guan,Caiwen Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出QFoldAgent，一个闭环多智能体框架，用于5残基四面体晶格蛋白质折叠。通过设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚项、VQE量子-经典管道优化哈密顿量、反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代优化惩罚系数。在QDockBank基准上，RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å；在未知序列上，结构有效性从87.5%提升至98.7%，87%的初始无效案例被恢复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 混合量子-经典蛋白质结构预测严重依赖哈密顿量惩罚权重，但现有晶格工作流程通常手动固定这些系数且仅在模拟中评估极短片段。需要一种自动化迭代方法来系统性地优化惩罚系数。

Method: 构建三智能体闭环框架：(1) 设计智能体提出序列条件惩罚系数；(2) VQE量子-经典管道在Qiskit Aer噪声下优化哈密顿量；(3) 反馈智能体利用能量景观诊断和MolProbity验证信号迭代改进惩罚系数。在55个QDockBank片段和100个覆盖优化未知序列上评估。

Result: QDockBank基准上中位RMSD从3.64Å降至3.20Å，最难目标提升最大。未知序列上结构有效性从87.5%升至98.7%，87%无效案例恢复。最强控制器在87%序列上改进cycle-3能量，同时保持96% Ramachandran偏好几何。

Conclusion: 迭代智能体控制能够系统性地改善优化行为并减少5残基量子设定中的失败案例。

Abstract: Hybrid quantum-classical protein structure prediction depends strongly on Hamiltonian penalty weights, yet existing lattice-based workflows typically fix these coefficients by hand and evaluate only very short fragments in simulation. We present QFoldAgent, a closed-loop multi-agent framework for 5-residue tetrahedral-lattice folding in which a design agent proposes sequence-conditioned penalties, a VQE-based quantum-classical pipeline optimizes the resulting Hamiltonian under Qiskit Aer noise, and a feedback agent uses energy-landscape diagnostics and MolProbity validation signals to refine penalties across cycles. Ground-truth metrics such as RMSD are never exposed to the agents and are used only for evaluation. We study the framework on two complementary datasets: 55 QDockBank-derived fragments with known structures and 100 coverage-optimized unseen sequences. On the QDockBank benchmark, QFoldAgent reduces median RMSD from 3.64 Å to 3.20 Å, with the largest gains on the hardest targets. On unseen sequences, the closed loop raises structural validity from 87.5% to 98.7%, recovers 87% of initially invalid cases, and the strongest controller improves cycle-3 energy on 87% of sequences while maintaining 96% Ramachandran-favored geometry. These results show that iterative agent control can systematically improve optimization behavior and reduce failure cases in a 5-residue quantum setting.

</details>


### [81] [Same Question, Different Answers: Evaluating LLM Reliability Beyond Accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22554)
*Kazem Faghih,Yize Cheng,Shoumik Saha,Mobina Pournemat,Armin Gerami,Soheil Feizi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 大型语言模型在不同但等价的表述下，其回答经常不一致：同一问题使用不同措辞时，模型会在正确与错误答案之间切换，不匹配率超过23%。虽然整体准确率变化不大，但单次提示的正确性并不可靠。研究表明模型内部存在潜在知识但检索不一致，通过简单的自释义策略可部分恢复这些知识并提升推理性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估LLM的基准测试通常只使用单一表述方式衡量模型准确率，无法揭示模型在等义表述下的回答稳定性。作者旨在探究模型知识是否被可靠地应用，以及单次提示正确性是否能反映真实可靠性。

Method: 在4个基准和13个模型上，对事实问答和数学推理任务进行保义改写，比较模型在原始表述和改写后表述下的输出一致性，并引入自释义策略进行干预。

Result: 模型输出高度依赖措辞，实例级不匹配率超过23%；单次正确提示往往不可靠；但多数问题至少有一种表述能生成正确答案，表明知识存在但检索不一致。

Conclusion: 标准准确率指标会掩盖实质性的不稳定性，评估等义输入下的一致性才能更清晰反映LLM的真实可靠性；自释义策略可部分恢复潜在知识。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often achieve strong accuracy on benchmarks, yet it remains unclear how reliably they apply this knowledge when the same question is phrased in different but equivalent ways. In this work, we study how model answers change under meaning-preserving paraphrases across factual question answering and mathematical reasoning tasks. Across four benchmarks and 13 models, we find that model outputs frequently depend on the exact wording of the prompt. While overall accuracy typically changes only modestly across paraphrases, instance-level behavior is far less stable: for many questions, models alternate between correct and incorrect answers depending on phrasing, with mismatch rates reaching more than 23%. Conditioning on questions that are answered correctly in their original form reveals even larger failures measured by answer flip rates, showing that single-prompt correctness is often a poor indicator of reliability. At the same time, we find that models often produce a correct answer for at least one paraphrase of a question, suggesting that the underlying knowledge is present but inconsistently retrieved. Building on this observation, we show that a simple self-paraphrasing strategy can partially recover this latent knowledge and improve performance at inference time. Together, these findings suggest that standard accuracy metrics can mask substantial instability, and that evaluating consistency across equivalent inputs provides a clearer picture of LLM reliability.

</details>


### [82] [DeepLens Diagnosis Agent: Agentic Workflow Design Lets a Small Reasoning Model Compete with Frontier LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22555)
*Mahmood Bayeshi,Veysel Kocaman,Muhammed Ali Naqvi,Yigit Gul,David Talby*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepLenz Diagnosis Agent，一种五阶段诊断推理管线，以小型医疗推理模型（JSL Medical Small 7B v2）和RAG为核心，在DiagnosisArena基准上实现60.14% top-1诊断准确率，相较无管线版本提升36个百分点，且成本仅$0.0072/例，低于Claude Sonnet 4.5和Gemini 3.1 Pro的同时性能超越它们。管线产生结构化中间产物，支持可审计与可溯源的错误定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有前沿LLM虽为强大的通用模型，但在医疗诊断这一多阶段推理任务中，单次提示（single-shot prompting）往往产生脆弱的诊断推理结果，缺乏结构化和过程约束，难以在高风险场景中保证可靠性和可审计性。

Method: 提出DeepLenz Diagnosis Agent，五阶段管线：（1）结构化临床提取（structured clinical extraction）；（2）受控检索（disciplined retrieval）；（3）受限候选生成（constrained candidate generation）；（4）显式证据三角验证（explicit evidence triangulation）；（5）可审计最终决策（auditable final decision）。核心使用小型医疗推理模型JSL Medical Small 7B v2与RAG。

Result: 在915例DiagnosisArena基准上，Top-1诊断准确率60.14%，超越所有中小型模型；无管线版本仅23.99%，提升36个百分点；成本$0.0072/例（24K tokens on A100），延迟24秒；比Claude Sonnet 4.5便宜35-45%且准确率高出9.70pp，比Gemini 3.1 Pro高出9.17pp。

Conclusion: 诊断推理在不确定性下不仅需要知识回忆，更需要过程约束；管线化设计可以纠正前沿模型失败，结构化中间产物支持高 stakes 场景下的可追踪性、可复现性和可审计性。

Abstract: Medical diagnosis is a multi-stage process: extract facts, consult knowledge, generate a differential analysis, and select the best diagnosis with explanations. Frontier LLMs are strong generalists, but single-shot prompting often yields brittle diagnostic reasoning. We present the DeepLens Diagnosis Agent, a five-stage harnessing pipeline (combining model capabilities with disciplined process constraints) centered on a small medical reasoning model (JSL Medical Small 7B v2) and retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). The pipeline enforces structured clinical extraction, disciplined retrieval, constrained candidate generation, explicit evidence triangulation, and an auditable final decision. On the 915-case DiagnosisArena benchmark, the agent achieved 60.14% top-1 diagnostic accuracy, the highest among small and medium-sized models. The same model without the agent workflow achieved 23.99%, a +36-point gain from workflow design alone, despite 88.2% on standard medical benchmarks, showing that diagnostic reasoning under uncertainty requires more than knowledge recall. The agent costs USD 0.0072 per case (24K tokens on A100) with 24-second latency, 35-45% cheaper than Claude Sonnet 4.5 (USD 0.0110) and Gemini 3.1 Pro (USD 0.0128) while outperforming them by +9.70pp and +9.17pp. Harnessing can also correct frontier model failures; workflow constraints can outweigh parameter count or API cost.
  Beyond aggregate accuracy, the pipeline produces structured intermediate artifacts that make each stage inspectable and support error localization. These properties support high-stakes settings where traceability, reproducibility, and auditable evidence matter alongside benchmark performance.

</details>


### [83] [MIITA: Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation for Continual Learning with Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22556)
*Dong Li,Yanchi Liu,Xujiang Zhao,Wei Cheng,Zhengzhang Chen,Xintao Wu,Zhong Chen,Chen Zhao,Haifeng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MIITA是一个面向资源受限场景下小语言模型（SLM）的监督持续学习框架。它通过存储监督经验为紧凑的修正方向原型与语义锚点，在推理时利用语义和不确定性线索检索，并通过门控临时隐藏状态适配实现非破坏性的旧知识复用，无需更新主干参数、扩展提示或测试时反向传播。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小语言模型（SLM）在资源受限部署中需持续适应演进需求，但直接更新有限参数空间会导致灾难性遗忘。现有基于记忆的方法专为大语言模型（LLM）设计，依赖充足存储和强上下文推理能力，而SLM不具备这些条件。

Method: 1) 将监督经验存储为紧凑的修正方向原型（compact correction-direction prototypes）与语义锚点（semantic anchors）；2) 推理时通过语义和不确定性线索（semantic and uncertainty-based cues）检索；3) 通过门控临时隐藏状态适配（gated temporary hidden-state adaptation）应用检索到的方向，实现非破坏性旧知识复用，无需主干更新、提示扩展或测试时反向传播。

Result: 在多种监督持续学习设置下的广泛实验表明，MIITA在固定记忆预算下一致地提升了最终性能并缓解了遗忘问题。

Conclusion: MIITA通过局部理论分析（一阶损失减少、不确定性引导检索、方向覆盖）与旧阶段知识保留相关联，验证了框架设计的合理性，为资源受限场景下的SLM持续学习提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Continual learning (CL) is essential for small language models (SLMs) to adapt to evolving real-world needs in resource-constrained deployments. However, directly updating their limited parameter space causes catastrophic forgetting. While memory-based methods naturally address this by decoupling knowledge retention from parameters, existing approaches designed for large language models (LLMs) rely on abundant storage and strong in-context reasoning that SLMs lack. To address these challenges, we propose MIITA, a Memory-Induced Inference-Time Adaptation framework for supervised CL under constrained storage. MIITA stores supervised experiences as compact correction-direction prototypes with semantic anchors, and retrieves them at inference time using semantic and uncertainty-based cues. The retrieved directions are applied through gated temporary hidden-state adaptation, enabling non-destructive reuse of past supervision without backbone updates, prompt extensions, or test-time backpropagation. A local theoretical analysis links this design to first-order loss reduction, uncertainty-guided retrieval, and directional coverage for retaining old-stage knowledge. Extensive experiments across diverse supervised CL settings show that MIITA consistently improves final performance and mitigates forgetting under fixed memory budgets.

</details>


### [84] [Codifying the Judge: Scalable Evaluation via Program Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22561)
*Tzu-Heng Huang,Shengqi Qiu,Frederic Sala*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出程序蒸馏方法，将LLM的评判逻辑蒸馏为可编程评判委员会，并构建PAJAMA系统，在降低成本和延迟的同时，实现了与13B规模LLM评判相当的性能，并在RewardBench上以两个数量级更低的成本超越专有LLM的奖励模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM-as-a-judge存在高成本、显著延迟和决策不透明的问题，限制了其可扩展性和可靠性，亟需一种更高效、可解释的替代方案。

Method: 提出程序蒸馏（program distillation）：将LLM的决策逻辑蒸馏为可编程评判委员会（programmatic judges），每个程序直接对候选对象打分；构建PAJAMA系统，包含程序合成、聚合决策和低置信度回退LLM的机制。

Result: 在5个数据集和4个模型家族上，可编程评判匹配13B规模LLM评判的性能；PAJAMA在准确率和吞吐量上提升，推进Pareto前沿；在RewardBench上，基于程序蒸馏的奖励模型以两个数量级更低的API成本超越专有LLM标签训练的模型。

Conclusion: 程序蒸馏是一种简单、高效且透明的LLM评估替代方案，能够在不牺牲性能的前提下大幅降低成本和延迟，并可用于生成有效的奖励信号。

Abstract: LLM-as-a-judge has become the standard for automated evaluation, but it suffers from high cost, significant latency, and opaque decisions -- limitations that undermine its scalability and reliability. We address these with a simple, efficient alternative: program distillation. Instead of prompting an LLM at the evaluation time, we distill its decision logic into a committee of programs that score candidates directly. These programmatic judges offer transparency, are easily inspected or edited, and eliminate per-sample API costs. Building on this notion, we introduce PAJAMA, a system that synthesizes programs as judges, aggregates their decisions into a joint verdict, and incorporates a fallback mechanism to selectively escalate low-confidence cases to an LLM. Across five datasets and four model families, we show that programmatic judges can match the performance of a 13B-size LLM judge. When using program outputs as routing signals, PAJAMA improves both accuracy and throughput and advances the Pareto frontier. Beyond evaluation, programmatic judges produce cheap and effective reward signals: on RewardBench, a reward model distilled from programs' verdicts outperforms one trained on a proprietary LLM's labels at two orders of magnitude lower API cost.

</details>


### [85] [SF-AMS: Strategic Forgetting for Structured Memory in LLM Agent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22562)
*Ning Yang,Siqi Li,Miaoxin Shen,Yuan Zhou,Meng Zhang,Tong Li,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SF-AMS 提出了一种基于效用驱动的遗忘机制，通过动态建模记忆单元的长程重要性，替代静态检索和启发式衰减，在多种长上下文推理任务上显著超越现有基线（如 LightMem、MemO、A-Mem），最高提升达 9.65 F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长上下文依赖是 LLM 智能体的主要瓶颈，冗余和无关信息会损害多步推理能力。现有静态检索和启发式衰减方法无法有效维护高效用记忆。

Method: 提出 Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS)，核心包括：(1) 效用驱动的记忆存活机制，从使用冗余性和时间信号更新记忆重要性；(2) 分层记忆结构，优先保留稳定实体一致信息并过滤噪声；(3) Composite Importance Scoring 整合语义和实体级信号以提升检索鲁棒性。

Result: 在 LoCoMo 和 LongMemEval-s 上一致超越 SOTA；多跳推理（Qwen2.5-7B）+9.65 F1，时序推理（GPT-4o-mini）+6.91 F1，开放域任务 +6.53 F1，跨主干模型泛化性强。

Conclusion: 将记忆重要性建模为动态效用信号对于可靠的长上下文推理至关重要。

Abstract: Managing long-context dependencies remains a primary bottleneck in LLM agents, as redundant and irrelevant information can degrade multi-step reasoning. Strategic Forgetting for Agent Memory Systems (SF-AMS) is proposed as a framework for maintaining compact high-utility memory by modeling the long-term importance of memory units. SF-AMS replaces static retrieval and heuristic decay with a utility-driven survival mechanism that updates memory importance from usage redundancy and temporal signals, inducing a hierarchical memory structure that prioritizes stable entity-consistent information while filtering noise. On top of this, Composite Importance Scoring integrates semantic and entity level signals to improve retrieval robustness. Experiments on LoCoMo and LongMemEval-s show consistent gains over strong state of the art baselines including LightMem MemO and A-Mem. The largest improvement appears in multi-hop reasoning under Qwen2.5-7B where SF-AMS achieves plus 9.65 F1 over the strongest baseline followed by temporal reasoning under GPT-4o-mini plus 6.91 F1 and open-domain tasks plus 6.53 F1 demonstrating strong cross backbone generalization. These results show that modeling memory importance as a dynamic utility signal is critical for reliable long-context reasoning.

</details>


### [86] [Synthetic Scenario Generation for Evaluation of Industry 4.0 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22563)
*Sagar Chethan Kumar,Rohith Kanathur,Dhaval Patel,Kaoutar El Maghraoui*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文针对工业智能体基准测试场景不足的问题，在AssetOpsBench基础上新增智能电网变压器资产类别及四种IEC诊断工具，并提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent管道用于合成场景生成，通过优化实现了8倍加速且保持场景质量（复合质量评分74.2 vs 73.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工业智能体基准测试（如AssetOpsBench）依赖人工编写场景，覆盖资产类别有限，缺乏可扩展的合成场景生成方法。

Method: 1. 扩展AssetOpsBench，新增智能电网变压器资产类及四种IEC诊断工具（健康指数预测、溶解气体分析、绕组温度评估、负载曲线评估）。2. 提出ScenarioGeneratorAgent管道：基于证据构建资产档案、覆盖感知场景预算分配、混合验证-修复循环（保证模式有效性、工具可达性、物理合理性、标准对齐、去重）。3. 优化策略：两级缓存、并行焦点组生成、线程池卸载、批量LLM调用、早期拒绝过滤。

Result: 在智能电网变压器场景生成中，优化后端到端运行时间降低8倍（生成50个场景），复合质量评分74.2±1.9，与未优化基线73.8±3.0相当，表明质量无损。

Conclusion: 基于标准约束的合成场景生成能够高效扩展工业智能体基准测试，且不牺牲场景质量，为工业基准的规模化扩展提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Industrial agent benchmarks require realistic evaluation scenarios that integrate telemetry, failure modes, maintenance records, and domain standards. However, existing benchmarks such as AssetOpsBench rely on manually authored scenarios and cover a limited set of asset classes. We extend AssetOpsBench with a Smart Grid Transformer asset class and four IEC-grounded diagnostic tools for health-index prediction, dissolved-gas analysis, winding-temperature assessment, and load-profile assessment. We further introduce ScenarioGeneratorAgent, a pipeline for synthetic industrial-agent scenario generation. The pipeline constructs evidence-grounded asset profiles, allocates coverage-aware scenario budgets across operational domains, and generates candidates through a hybrid validation-and-repair loop that enforces schema validity, tool reachability, physical plausibility, standards alignment, and deduplication. To improve scalability, we apply two-level caching, parallel focus-group generation, thread-pool offloading, batched LLM calls, and early rejection filtering. On Smart Grid Transformer scenario generation, these optimizations reduce end-to-end runtime by $8\times$ for 50 scenarios while preserving quality, achieving a composite quality score of $74.2 \pm 1.9$ compared with $73.8 \pm 3.0$ for the unoptimized baseline. These results show that standards-grounded synthetic scenario generation can efficiently expand industrial-agent benchmarks without sacrificing scenario quality.

</details>


### [87] [Loss-Aware Feature-Map Pruning in Convolutional Neural Networks Using Multi-Armed Bandits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22564)
*Salem Ameen,Sunil Vadera*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多臂老虎机的损失感知特征图剪枝框架，通过将每个候选特征图视为一个臂，在有限播放预算内评估并永久移除冗余特征图及其对应的滤波器、偏置和下一层输入通道核，从而在保持模型精度的同时减少计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卷积神经网络中存在大量冗余特征图，增加了存储和推理成本，需一种结构化的剪枝方法去除冗余特征图及其对应的滤波器，而非仅剪除孤立标量权重。

Method: 提出损失感知特征图剪枝框架：将每个候选特征图视为多臂老虎机的一个臂；每次播放时临时屏蔽一个特征图并在采样小批数据上评估损失变化，然后恢复该图并将观测到的损失变化转换为安全移除奖励；在固定播放预算后，根据学习得分对候选图排序，永久移除前k个特征图及其滤波器、偏置和对应下一层输入通道核。使用UCB1和Thompson Sampling进行策略选择。

Result: 在LeNet/MNIST、CIFAR-10、CIFAR-100、SVHN、CUB-200-2011和Oxford Flowers 102数据集上验证，UCB1和Thompson Sampling在移除特征图并减少卷积计算量的同时，保持了与未剪枝模型接近的准确率。Friedman和Nemenyi检验显示UCB1获得最高平均排名，Thompson Sampling次之，两者均显著优于贪婪和基于幅度的剪枝方法，且与原始未剪枝模型在统计上无显著差异。

Conclusion: 基于多臂老虎机的损失感知剪枝框架能有效识别并移除冗余特征图，在保证模型精度的前提下显著降低计算成本，其中UCB1策略表现最佳。

Abstract: Convolutional neural networks often contain redundant feature maps that increase storage and inference cost. This paper presents a loss-aware feature-map pruning framework using multi-armed bandits. Feature-map pruning is structured because it removes complete convolutional output channels and their producing filters rather than isolated scalar weights. Each candidate feature map is treated as an arm. At each play time, one map is temporarily masked and evaluated on a sampled mini-batch; the map is then restored and the observed loss change is converted into a safe-removal reward. After a fixed play budget, candidate maps are ranked by learned scores and the top-k maps are permanently removed with their filters, biases and corresponding next-layer input-channel kernels. The study evaluates UCB1 and Thompson Sampling, compares them with direct/oracle-style evaluation on LeNet/MNIST, and extends the evaluation to MNIST, CIFAR-10, CIFAR-100, SVHN, CUB-200-2011 and Oxford Flowers 102. Results show that UCB1 and Thompson Sampling preserve accuracy close to unpruned models while removing feature maps and reducing convolutional computation. Friedman and Nemenyi tests show that UCB1 obtains the highest mean rank, followed by Thompson Sampling; both significantly outperform greedy and magnitude-based pruning while remaining statistically comparable to the original unpruned model.

</details>


### [88] [DSTFView: Multi-View Cloud-Edge Workload Forecasting with Dual-Input Spatio-Temporal-Frequency Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22565)
*Qingzhong Li,Hui Ma,Yajun Zhang,Qingchang Ma,Zhou Long*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了DSTFView，一种用于云边协同环境的双输入时空频多视角工作负载预测框架，通过联合建模邻近性与周期性依赖、提取时空频域特征以及自适应融合机制，在CPU和TP数据集上优于现有基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘端AI推理广泛部署后，边缘平台需要支持延迟敏感、高并发和高可靠性应用，但现有方法难以在云边协同环境中平衡多维特征建模与预测效率。

Method: 提出DSTFView框架，包含双输入结构（邻近性与周期性依赖）、时空频域特征提取模块，以及自适应融合机制来动态调整各视角贡献以捕捉突变。

Result: 在CPU和TP数据集上，DSTFView在多个预测时间跨度和评估指标上一致优于代表性基线方法。

Conclusion: DSTFView能有效解决云边协同环境下的工作负载预测难题，在平衡多维特征建模与预测效率方面效果显著。

Abstract: With the widespread deployment of edge-side AI inference, edge platforms are increasingly required to support latency-sensitive, highly concurrent, and reliability-critical applications. However, existing methods often struggle to balance multidimensional feature modeling and forecasting efficiency in collaborative cloud-edge environments. To address this issue, we propose DSTFView, a dual-input spatio-temporal-frequency multi-view workload forecasting framework for collaborative cloud-edge environments. It jointly models closeness and period dependencies and extracts spatial, temporal, and frequency-domain dependencies. Besides, it designs an adaptive fusion mechanism and adjusts the contribution of each view to capture abrupt changes. Experimental results on the CPU and TP datasets demonstrate that DSTFView consistently outperforms representative baselines across multiple forecasting horizons and evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [89] [MedLoCoMo: A Long-Context Multi-Session Medical Dialogue Benchmark for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22566)
*Zeyu Zhang,Ziqing Wang,Kaize Ding*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MedLoCoMo是一个面向多入院医疗对话中患者特定临床推理的长上下文医学记忆基准，包含100条患者时间线（平均每段对话1,669.8轮、29.7次就诊、74,512.2个token），涵盖单次入院、跨入院和对抗性不可回答三类场景。实验表明跨入院推理比局部证据使用更难，即使模型拥有长上下文窗口或外部检索机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学QA基准主要测试短上下文知识或单个文档的 grounding，缺乏评估LLM在纵向患者病史中进行信息使用、跨入院连接以及合理拒答能力的基准。

Method: 基于MIMIC-IV和MIMIC-IV-Note的去标识化记录，构建入院级别的临床包，合成医患对话，生成单次入院、跨入院和对抗性不可回答三类场景的证据关联QA样本。

Result: 基准包含100条患者时间线，平均每段对话1,669.8轮、29.7次就诊、74,512.2个token；跨入院推理在所有基线中一致比局部证据使用更困难。

Conclusion: 现有模型（包括长上下文窗口模型及使用外部记忆或检索方法的模型）在跨入院推理方面仍存在显著不足，需要进一步提升对纵向患者病史的推理能力。

Abstract: MedLoCoMo is a Medical Long-Context Memory benchmark for patient-specific clinical reasoning over multi-admission medical dialogue. Existing medical QA benchmarks largely test short context knowledge or single document grounding, leaving open whether LLMs can use, connect, and abstain over longitudinal patient histories. We build MedLoCoMo from deidentified MIMIC-IV and MIMIC-IV-Note records by constructing admission-level clinical packets, synthesizing grounded doctor-patient conversations, and generating evidence linked QA items over single-admission, cross-admission, and adversarial unanswerable settings. The benchmark contains 100 patient timelines averaging 1,669.8 turns, 29.7 sessions, and 74,512.2 tokens per conversation. Across the evaluated baselines, cross-admission reasoning is consistently harder than localized evidence use, even when models have long context windows or use external memory or retrieval methods. The code and MedLoCoMo benchmark release is available at https://github.com/leozzy13/MedLoCoMo for use and reproducibility.

</details>


### [90] [Keyword Matters: Unveiling the Energy Sensitivity of On-Device LLM Prompting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22568)
*Ruiyi Tao,Xiaolong Tu,Haoxin Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过智能手机上的真实功耗测量，实证研究了提示词设计（特别是关键词和指令结构）对端侧部署的大语言模型能耗的影响，发现提示工程可作为提升能效的轻量级手段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在移动和嵌入式设备上部署时面临电池供电硬件的高能耗限制，而现有研究主要关注模型压缩和运行时加速，提示词设计对能效的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 在智能手机上对端侧大语言模型进行真实功耗测量，通过改变提示词中的祈使关键词和指令结构，量化语言特征对解码长度和总能耗的影响。

Result: 不同动词和任务之间的能耗存在一致的差异，表明提示工程能够有效影响端侧推理的能源消耗。

Conclusion: 提示词设计是提升端侧大语言模型能效的一个轻量级、可行的优化方向，值得进一步探索。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed on mobile and embedded devices to improve privacy and reduce network latency. Yet on-device inference faces a fundamental constraint: high energy consumption on battery-powered, resource-limited hardware. While model compression and runtime acceleration have been widely studied, the effect of \emph{prompt design} on energy efficiency remains underexplored. This paper presents an empirical study of the relationship between prompt wording and energy consumption for on-device LLMs. Using real power measurements collected on a smartphone, we quantify how linguistic features, particularly imperative keywords and instruction structure, affect decoding length and total energy. Our results show consistent energy differences across verbs and tasks, indicating that prompt engineering is a lightweight lever for improving energy efficiency.

</details>


### [91] [Execution-Grounded Security Testing for Coding Agents in Software Engineering Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22569)
*Yifei Ge,Weisong Sun,Jinkun Xiao,Yuchen Chen,Yebo Feng,Peizhuo Lv,Xia Feng,Chunrong Fang,Zhihong Zhao,Zhenyu Chen,Yang Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于执行证据的红队测试框架，通过将不安全操作伪装进常规软件工程任务（如单元测试、回归测试等），测试编码智能体在系统运维中的执行层安全边界。实验发现，编码智能体在任务伪装下极易执行危险操作（代码载体达73.61%，文本载体达53.93%），表明当前系统运维场景下的编码智能体仍存在严重安全隐患。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 编码智能体在系统运维中可通过工具调用直接修改项目工件、执行环境和底层系统，且更改可能持久化并被滥用。现有安全测试多关注智能体"说了什么"，而非其对周围环境"实际做了什么"，缺乏对执行层安全边界的系统化测试方法。

Method: 提出执行锚定的红队测试框架，利用可观察的沙箱证据（包括工具调用、运行时追踪、文件系统差异）来探测执行层安全边界。将目标不安全操作嵌入常规软件工程工作负载（单元测试、回归测试、崩溃复现、验证），并使用执行预言机在初始探测被拒绝或失败时指导细化。

Result: 在多个智能体框架和模型骨干上，红队工作负载重公式化显著提高了经验证的不安全执行率，代码载体达73.61%，文本载体达53.93%。

Conclusion: 编码智能体在系统运维中在任务伪装下仍然不安全，一旦风险意图隐藏在看似合理的工程任务中，智能体就会被诱导对周围系统执行不安全操作。编码智能体在系统运维中仍需要更强的安全测试和防护措施。

Abstract: Coding agents are increasingly integrated into system operations, where their tool use can directly modify project artifacts, execution environments, and the underlying system. For example, if a coding agent inserts a hook into a system startup or configuration script, that change can persist after the interaction, be triggered later, and abuse delegated user or system privileges to modify the system. This makes security testing a system problem: the key question is not only what the agent says, but what it actually does to the surrounding environment. We present an execution-grounded red-team testing framework for probing this execution-layer security boundary using observable sandbox evidence, including tool invocations, runtime traces, and file-system diffs. Our framework embeds target unsafe operations into routine software engineering workloads, including unit testing, regression testing, crash reproduction, and validation, and uses an execution oracle to guide refinement when an initial probe is rejected or fails. Across multiple agent frameworks and model backbones, our red-team workload reformulation substantially increases verified unsafe execution, reaching 73.61% on code carriers and 53.93% on text carriers. These results show that coding agents in system operations remain insecure under task disguise: once risky intent is hidden inside plausible engineering tasks, the agent can be induced to carry out unsafe actions on the surrounding system. More broadly, coding agents in system operations still demand stronger security testing and safeguards.

</details>


### [92] [Reference Feature Atlases for Mechanistic Auditing of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22570)
*Rui Wu,Tong Che*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出参考特征图谱（Reference Feature Atlas）：在参考模型组上一次性训练稀疏特征库，新模型仅需拟合线性解码器即可复用。残差通道能显式检测“参考范围外”的特征，在隐藏目标检测和可控性上优于传统SAE和交叉编码器基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 审计新语言模型通常需要从头重新学习其内部特征，成本高且缺乏跨模型的统一坐标系。

Method: 在参考模型组上训练稀疏特征库（图谱通道），新目标模型通过拟合线性解码器复用；同时保留残差通道，仅学习图谱无法重建的特征，作为“参考范围外”的显式审计信号。

Result: 在5个7-9B指令微调模型上训练留一法图谱，审计Mistral和Qwen目标。残差通道使隐藏目标完全可控且不影响匹配控制项，在Mistral上优于重训的SAE和交叉编码器基线；在Qwen-2.5上还发现了与参考模型组相对的政治框架聚类，对其进行干预可改变审计指标。

Conclusion: 参考特征图谱提供了一种跨模型可复用的高效审计框架，残差通道能显式检测新模型中的新颖特征，在可控性和可解释性上优于传统方法。

Abstract: Auditing a new language model usually means relearning and reinterpreting its internal features from scratch. We propose a reference feature atlas: a sparse feature library trained once on a reference panel and reused for new targets, which attach by fitting only a linear decoder. This yields two complementary views. The atlas channel reads the target on already interpreted panel features, providing a stable coordinate system across models. The residual channel learns features only from what the atlas fails to reconstruct, making "outside the reference panel" an explicit audit signal.
  We train leave-one-out atlases over five 7-9B instruction-tuned models and audit held-out Mistral and Qwen targets. On three controlled LoRA hidden objectives injected into both targets, the residual channel makes the planted mechanism perfectly controllable at runtime while matched controls stay unaffected and recovers the planted objective as the top-ranked latent across both targets; on Mistral, where the per-target SAE and pairwise crosscoder baselines are retrained for a head-to-head benchmark, both baselines fail to do so. On Qwen-2.5, the same channel additionally reveals a panel-relative political-framing cluster; steering it shifts the audited framing metrics while out-of-domain controls remain unchanged.

</details>


### [93] [SCAIR: Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning for Enterprise Knowledge Graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22571)
*Prateek Chaturvedi,Yuqicheng Zhu,Hongkuan Zhou,Dongzhuoran Zhou,Yunjie He,Steffen Staab,Fei Du,Jie Tang,Evgeny Kharlamov*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCAIR是一个无需训练的框架，通过注入模式条件结构先验和强制执行模式感知遍历，显著提升了企业知识图谱上的检索增强生成性能，证明企业图推理必须显式融入目标领域的结构和操作约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于智能体的KG-RAG方法在公开基准上表现良好，但无法泛化到真实企业的密集、模式驱动且操作受限的知识图谱，需要一种能融入领域结构约束的解决方案。

Method: 提出SCAIR框架，将结构化规划与受控迭代推理相结合，通过注入模式条件结构先验并强制执行模式感知遍历，实现多跳推理中的领域约束对齐。

Result: 在基于真实CMDB构建的企业级基准上，SCAIR显著优于现有KG-RAG方法，无需模型重训练即可获得实质性性能提升。

Conclusion: 可靠的企业图推理不能依赖通用智能体设计，必须显式融入目标领域的结构和操作约束；将智能体设计与业务逻辑对齐可以在无需昂贵模型重训练的前提下获得显著收益。

Abstract: Knowledge Graph-based Retrieval-Augmented Generation (KG-RAG) enables natural language interaction with structured enterprise knowledge, yet existing agentic approaches that perform well on public benchmarks often fail to generalize to real-world enterprise Knowledge Graphs (KGs), which are dense, schema-driven, and operationally constrained. To address these limitations, we propose SCAIR (Schema-Conditioned Agentic Iterative Reasoning), a training-free framework that integrates structured planning with controlled iterative reasoning by injecting schema-conditioned structural priors and enforcing schema-aware traversal during multi-hop reasoning. Experiments on an enterprise-oriented benchmark constructed from a real-world Configuration Management DataBase (CMDB) demonstrate that SCAIR substantially improves performance over existing KG-RAG methods. Crucially, our study highlights that reliable enterprise graph reasoning cannot rely on generic agentic designs; instead, it must explicitly incorporate the target domain's structural and operational constraints into the reasoning process. We demonstrate that by aligning agent design with business logic, substantial performance gains can be achieved without the need for costly model retraining.

</details>


### [94] [Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL): Live Schema Grounding and Hallucination Validation for Oracle NL2SQL](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22572)
*Sanjay Mishra,Divya Chukkapalli,Ganesh R. Naik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL)，一种无需模型重训练的轻量级中间件，通过实时查询Oracle数据字典、注入真实模式信息到LLM提示中，并结合Hallucination Index (Hidx)进行引用验证与自动修正，在TPC-H基准上将Oracle NL2SQL的执行成功率从2.2%提升至62.6%，执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在真实企业Oracle数据库上执行NL2SQL时，常因列名、别名的幻觉以及方言语法错误导致执行失败（如ORA-00904无效标识符错误），根本原因在于模型缺乏对数据库真实模式的认知。

Method: SAL作为轻量级中间件，包含两个核心组件：1) Schema Grounding：实时查询Oracle的USER_TAB_COLUMNS目录构建动态模式映射，根据问题选择相关子集注入LLM提示；2) Hallucination Index (Hidx)：验证生成SQL中所有alias.column引用是否存在于真实目录中，自动重写可预测的前缀错误，否则触发结构化重试。

Result: 在500个TPC-H自然语言问题、Oracle ADB 23c实例、GPT-4o-mini上评估：无模式接地时EGT仅2.2%；手动静态模式提示达62.0%；SAL无需手动模式配置达62.6% EGT（简单96%、中等95%、复杂40.7%），执行失败率从97.6%降至2.6%。

Conclusion: SAL通过实时模式接地与引用验证，有效解决了LLM在真实Oracle数据库NL2SQL中的模式幻觉问题，无需模型重训练即可大幅提升执行成功率，具有实际部署价值。

Abstract: Large language models can generate fluent SQL from natural language, but on real enterprise Oracle databases they frequently fail at execution time: columns and aliases are hallucinated and dialect-specific syntax is missed, leading to ORA-00904 invalid-identifier errors. In this setting, failures are primarily due to missing schema grounding: the model cannot know which tables and columns actually exist. This paper introduces Schema-Aware Localisation (SAL), a lightweight middleware layer for Oracle NL2SQL that requires no model retraining. SAL queries Oracle's USER_TAB_COLUMNS catalog to build a live schema map, selects a relevant table subset for each question (falling back to the full schema for multi-table queries), and injects this ground-truth context into the LLM prompt. Generated SQL is then checked by the Hallucination Index (Hidx), which validates every alias.column reference against the live catalog, automatically rewrites predictable prefix errors, and otherwise triggers a structured retry with itemised corrections. We evaluate SAL on 500 TPC-H natural language questions executed against a live Oracle Autonomous Database 23c instance using GPT-4o-mini. Without any schema grounding, execution-grounded truth (EGT; executes and matches the reference result set) is 2.2% (12/500). A hand-written static schema hint brings EGT to 62.0%. SAL, with no manual schema curation, achieves 62.6% EGT (96% simple, 95% medium, 40.7% complex) while reducing execution failures from 97.6% to 2.6%.

</details>


### [95] [PhononBench-MP40: a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset for phonon stability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22573)
*Wen-Kao Li,Ze-Feng Gao,Zhong-Yi Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了PhononBench-MP40，一个基于Materials Project数据库的声子稳定性基准数据集，包含46,899条完成记录（16,683稳定/30,216不稳定）及1,067条弛豫失败记录，并提供本地声子YAML谱、稳定性标签、最低采样频率等核心数据，旨在为声子稳定性分类、阈值研究和失败感知分流提供可审计的参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虚拟声子模式是计算材料筛选中的实际瓶颈，因为看似合理的结构在特定工作流下可能存在局部动态不稳定性。现有数据集缺乏工作流定义的声子稳定性标签和完整的频谱信息，亟需一个可审计、可复现的基准数据集来支持稳定性分类和阈值研究。

Method: 从Materials Project的47,969个MP40工作流任务出发，通过声子计算流程（phonopy）生成YAML格式的声子谱，从中提取稳定性标签、最低采样频率，并对完成和失败案例进行分离处理，构建了包含46,899条完整记录和1,067条弛豫失败记录的数据集。

Result: 获得46,899条已完成记录，其中16,683条标记为稳定，30,216条标记为不稳定（完成声子计算但存在虚频）；另有1,067条弛豫失败记录单独报告。数据集核心为本地YAML声子谱，稳定性标签和最低频率均源于此谱。

Conclusion: PhononBench-MP40为工作流定义的稳定性分类、最低频率分析、阈值研究和失败感知分流提供了可审计的参考基准，同时明确了参考工作流、数据模式和解释边界。

Abstract: Imaginary phonon modes remain a practical bottleneck in computational materials screening because otherwise plausible structures can be locally dynamically unstable under a chosen workflow. Here we present PhononBench-MP40, a spectrum-resolved benchmark dataset of Materials Project-derived crystals for workflow-defined phonon stability. The dataset starts from 47,969 MP40 workflow tasks and provides 46,899 completed records with paired stability labels and local phonopy YAML spectra, including 16,683 Stable records and 30,216 completed-phonon unstable records. A further 1,067 relaxation failures are reported separately rather than merged into the completed phonon denominator. The release centers on the local YAML spectrum: the stability label, the lowest sampled frequency and any threshold-dependent relabeling are derived from that spectrum. The dataset is openly available through Science Data Bank at https://doi.org/10.57760/sciencedb.38735. A companion GitHub repository provides the calculation code and lightweight access utilities. PhononBench-MP40 provides an auditable reference for workflow-defined stability classification, minimum-frequency analysis, threshold studies and failure-aware triage, while keeping the reference workflow, data schema and interpretation boundaries explicit.

</details>


### [96] [Too much evidence, too little time: From text to actionable recommendations through multi-objective evidence reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22574)
*Adela Bara,Simona-Vasilica Oprea*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SCEPTER是一个将临床病例描述转化为循证建议的框架，结合PubMed检索、语义排序、大模型、证据加权、矛盾检测、共识分析和多目标Pareto选择，在150个病例中将576篇论文压缩至53篇、7个Pareto最优主张和3条最终建议（压缩比192:1），同时保持高证据多样性（熵=0.901）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 循证临床决策需要专家识别、评估和整合相关科学文献，但PubMed检索复杂临床病例常返回数百篇文献，在时间限制下无法人工全部审阅。

Method: SCEPTER框架整合了PubMed检索、PubMedBERT语义排序、基于大语言模型的声明提取、证据水平加权、矛盾检测、共识分析和多目标Pareto主张选择，并包含Paper Q&A模块支持交互式文献探索。

Result: 在150个病例评估中，平均搜索空间从576篇论文缩减至53篇保留论文、7个Pareto最优主张和3条最终建议，总体压缩比192:1，保留证据多样性高（熵=0.901）。消融实验表明Pareto选择相比传统排序方法提升了证据多样性和推荐效用。

Conclusion: SCEPTER框架引入了多目标推理模型，将文献支持、矛盾分析和交互式文献查询整合为统一的临床决策支持流水线，有效压缩文献搜索空间的同时保持了证据多样性与推荐质量。

Abstract: Evidence-based clinical decision making requires specialists to identify, evaluate and synthesize relevant scientific literature. However, PubMed searches for complex clinical cases often return hundreds of publications that cannot be reviewed manually under time constraints. This study proposes SCEPTER (Single-Case Evidence-driven PubMed-To-rEcommendation Reasoner), a framework for transforming clinical case descriptions into evidence-based recommendations. SCEPTER combines PubMed retrieval, PubMedBERT semantic ranking, large language model (LLM)-based claim extraction, evidence-level weighting, contradiction detection, consensus analysis and multi-objective Pareto claim selection. The framework generates structured evidence syntheses and grounded actionable recommendations. A Paper Q&A module further enables interactive exploration of selected publications. The proposed framework introduces multi-objective reasoning model that integrates literature support, contradiction analysis and interactive literature interrogation into a unified clinical decision-support pipeline. Evaluation on 150 case studies demonstrated that the framework reduced an average search space of 576 papers to 53 retained papers, 7 Pareto-optimal claims and 3 final recommendations, corresponding to an overall compression ratio of 192:1. Despite this reduction, the retained evidence maintained high diversity (entropy=0.901). The ablation study showed that Pareto-based selection increased evidence diversity and recommendation utility compared with conventional ranking approaches.

</details>


<div id='cs.IT'></div>

# cs.IT [[Back]](#toc)

### [97] [A uniqueness theorem for the variational free energy decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22710)
*Michael P. Rubin*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明，在特定正则性条件下，唯一满足 $\log Z = -G + \Delta$ 精确分解（其中 $\Delta$ 仅依赖于 $Q$ 和 $\pi$，非负且当 $Q=\pi$ 时为零，$G$ 可加分离为参考测度和权重两项）的泛函对 $(G,\Delta)$ 必然是变分自由能 $F$ 和相对熵 $D(\cdot\|\pi)$。该结果唯一刻画了分解形式而非散度本身，与 Shore-Johnson、Csiszár 和 Amari 的公理化刻画互补。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 变分自由能 $F(Q;p,\ell)=D(Q\|p)-E_Q[\log\ell]$ 满足 $\log Z = -F + D(Q\|\pi)$ 的精确恒等式，而基于 $\alpha$-散度或 Rényi 散度的变分目标只能给出 $\log Z$ 的界而非等式。这引发了一个根本问题：哪些泛函能允许这种精确分解？本文旨在刻画满足该分解性质的泛函对的唯一形式。

Method: 作者将问题归约为假设条件：设 $(G,\Delta)$ 满足 $\log Z = -G + \Delta$，其中 $\Delta$ 是 $Q$ 和 $\pi$ 的函数，非负且仅当 $Q=\pi$ 时为零；$G$ 关于参考测度和权重可加分离。通过将假设简化为从正函数乘法群到 $(\mathbb{R},+)$ 的同态，并利用相对熵在最小值点的二阶消失性质，证明该同态必为零，从而唯一确定 $G=F$ 和 $\Delta=D(\cdot\|\pi)$。同时给出反例说明每个假设的必要性。

Result: 在所述正则性条件下，满足分解恒等式的泛函对 $(G,\Delta)$ 唯一确定为变分自由能 $F$ 和相对熵 $D(\cdot\|\pi)$。该刻画针对的是分解形式而非散度本身，因此与相对熵的经典公理化刻画（Shore-Johnson、Csiszár、Amari）互补——既不使用也不推广这些结果。

Conclusion: 变分自由能的 $\log Z = -F + D(Q\|\pi)$ 分解在所述意义下是唯一的，这解释了为何 $\alpha$-散度和 Rényi 散度无法产生类似的精确恒等式。该结果从分解角度提供了相对熵独特性的新视角，补充了已有的散度公理化理论。

Abstract: For a finite system with reference measure $p$ and positive weight $\ell$, the variational free energy $F(Q;p,\ell)=D(Q\,\|\,p)-E_Q[\log\ell]$ satisfies the exact identity $\log Z(p,\ell)=-F(Q;p,\ell)+D(Q\,\|\,π)$, where $Z$ is the partition function and $π$ the associated Gibbs measure. For a uniform reference measure this is the Gibbs-Bogoliubov inequality of mean-field theory; for a Bayesian model it is the evidence decomposition of variational inference. Variational objectives built from $α$- or Rényi divergences retain useful bounds on $\log Z$ but not identities of this form, which raises the question of which functionals admit an exact decomposition. We prove the following characterization. Suppose a pair $(G,Δ)$ satisfies $\log Z=-G+Δ$ with $Δ$ a function of $Q$ and $π$ alone; suppose $G$ is additively separable into a term depending on the reference measure and a term depending on the weight, with mild regularity; and suppose $Δ$ is nonnegative and vanishes precisely at $Q=π$. Then $G=F$ and $Δ=D(\cdot\,\|\,π)$. The proof reduces the hypotheses to a homomorphism from the multiplicative group of positive functions into $(\mathbb{R},+)$ and removes it using the second-order vanishing of relative entropy at its minimum. Counterexamples show that each hypothesis is needed. The result characterizes the decomposition rather than the divergence, and is therefore complementary to the axiomatic characterizations of relative entropy due to Shore-Johnson, Csiszár and Amari, which it neither uses nor extends.

</details>


### [98] [Operations on binary linear codes and their associated graphs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22800)
*John H. Castillo,Lisbeth D. Delgado-Ordoñez,Alexander Holguín-Villa*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文探讨了编码理论与图论之间的关联，以二元线性码的哈斯图Γ(C)为基础，刻画了扩展码、凿孔码以及两个码的直和所对应的图结构，建立了码运算图与特定图运算之间的关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立编码理论中码运算（扩展、凿孔、直和）与图论中图结构之间的形式化联系，利用二元线性码的哈斯图作为桥梁，揭示两类运算之间的对应关系。

Method: 以二元线性码的哈斯图Γ(C)（通过偏序关系表示码的陪集）为出发点，分别针对扩展码、凿孔码和直和三种码运算，分析并刻画其对应的图结构特征，并与图论中的特定图运算建立对应关系。

Result: 给出了扩展码、凿孔码和直和码对应的图的显式刻画，并建立了码运算图与图论中特定图运算之间的关联。

Conclusion: 成功建立了编码理论中若干基本码运算与图论中图结构操作之间的对应关系，为两个领域的交叉研究提供了新的视角。

Abstract: This manuscript addresses a connection between operations in coding theory and graph theory, as well as some operations in both areas. The concept of the Hasse diagram of a binary linear code, $Γ(C)$, is recall; this concept visually represents the cosets of a binary linear code with a partial ordering. The main objetive is to give a characterization of the graphs corresponding to: extended codes, punctured codes, and the direct sum of two codes, using the graph of the given binary linear code as a starting point. Relationships are established between the graphs of code operations and particular operations of graphs.

</details>


### [99] [Cyclotomy, External Difference Families, and Algebraic Manipulation Detection Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22960)
*Minfeng Shao,Miwako Mishima*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究有限域上基于分圆类的外部差族（EDF）构造，推导了充要条件，给出了偶块大小≤14时的显式判据，并利用分圆类、有限域直积和整数剩余环上的广义分圆类构造了有界广义强外部差族，从而得到无限族参数灵活的G-优强AMD码。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 外部差族与代数操作检测（AMD）码密切相关，AMD码是一种能保护消息免受对手加法篡改的密码学原语。本文旨在通过分圆类构造新的EDF及其推广结构，以生成更优的弱AMD码和强AMD码。

Method: 1. 在有限域上利用分圆类构造外部部分差族，推导其为EDF的充要条件；2. 针对偶块大小≤14，基于二次剩余和四次剩余给出显式判据；3. 利用有限域上的分圆类、有限域直积以及整数剩余环上的广义分圆类，构造有界广义强外部差族。

Result: 获得了偶块大小≤14的R-优弱AMD码；通过多种分圆类构造得到了无限族参数灵活的G-优强AMD码。

Conclusion: 本文提出的构造方法能系统性地生成具有灵活参数的最优AMD码，丰富了EDF理论及其在密码学中的应用。

Abstract: External difference families (EDFs) are closely related to algebraic manipulation detection (AMD) codes, a cryptographic primitive that protects messages against additive tampering by an adversary who cannot observe the transmitted codeword. We first study a cyclotomic construction of external partial difference families over finite fields and derive a necessary and sufficient condition under which the resulting families are EDFs. For even block sizes up to $14$, we obtain explicit criteria in terms of quadratic and biquadratic residues, yielding new $R$-optimal weak AMD codes. We then construct bounded generalized strong external difference families using cyclotomic classes over finite fields, direct products of finite fields, and generalized cyclotomic classes over integer residue rings. These constructions give infinite families of systematic $G$-optimal strong AMD codes with flexible parameters.

</details>


### [100] [Electromagnetic Neural Network for Direction-of-Arrival Estimation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23021)
*Shining Lin,Jiancheng An,Lu Gan,Victor C. M. Leung,Mehdi Bennis,Mérouane Debbah,Tie Jun Cui*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于电磁神经网络（EMNN）的无人机通信系统DOA估计方法，通过堆叠智能超表面（SIM）在电磁域进行超低功耗、超高速信号处理，结合分层估计框架（粗估计+精估计），在双信号场景下相比传统波束成形方法实现了约13dB的分类误差降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有高精度DOA估计算法在无人机机载信号处理约束下计算复杂度过高，难以实现实时处理。

Method: 提出由堆叠智能超表面（SIM）和全连接层组成的电磁神经网络（EMNN）；SIM每个超原子作为人工神经元在电磁域处理信号；采用分层DOA估计框架（粗估计+精估计两阶段），每阶段使用随机生成的训练样本及其对应频谱训练EMNN。

Result: 在双信号场景下，EMNN相比传统波束成形（CBF）方法实现了约13dB的分类误差降低，且具有更低的成本和射频相关功耗。

Conclusion: EMNN结合电磁域计算与分层估计框架，在降低计算复杂度和观测快拍数的同时实现了高精度DOA估计，适合无人机平台部署。

Abstract: Accurate and real-time direction of arrival (DOA) estimation is crucial for beamforming in unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) communication systems. However, the existing high-precision DOA estimation algorithms encounter high computational complexity when implemented on a UAV with on-board signal processing constraints. To tackle this issue, an electromagnetic neural network (EMNN) is developed for DOA estimation, which is capable of generating the angular spectrum of the incident signal based solely on amplitude observation. Specifically, the proposed EMNN consists of two components: a stacked intelligent metasurfaces (SIM) is mounted on the UAV, and each meta-atom is an artificial neuron that can process signals in the electromagnetic domain with low energy consumption and ultra-fast computing speed. Furthermore, a fully connected layer is cascaded to process the received amplitude signal, enhancing the non-linear extraction and representational ability of EMNN. Moreover, to reduce the computational complexity and observation snapshots required for high-resolution DOA estimation, we develop a hierarchical DOA estimation framework, which involves two stages for conducting coarse and fine DOA estimation, respectively. For each stage, EMNN is trained on randomly generated training samples and their corresponding spectra to achieve the desired estimation goal. Finally, the simulation results validate that the proposed EMNN achieves approximately 13 dB gain in classification error reduction over the conventional beamforming (CBF) method in dual-signal scenarios, albeit its lower cost and radio frequency (RF)-related power consumption.

</details>


### [101] [Multi-User Diversity Scaling in Heavy-Tailed Fading](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23135)
*Yonathan Murin,Ali Özer Ercan,Nariman Farsad*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明，在Fisher-Snedecor ℱ复合衰落信道下，多用户分集增益从经典的log₂log₂K对数-对数增长，转变为(1/m_s)log₂K的多项式增长，其中m_s为阴影衰落参数。该增益在干扰受限的泊松网络中仍然保持，并在中度至重度阴影衰落（m_s≤3）场景中最为显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典多用户分集理论预测吞吐量随用户数K呈log₂log₂K增长，但该结论基于轻尾衰落信道假设。在包含阴影衰落的复合衰落场景下，信道功率尾部行为发生变化，可能导致完全不同的容量缩放规律。本文旨在揭示并严格证明这种缩放规律的转变。

Method: 采用Fisher-Snedecor ℱ复合衰落模型，利用极值理论分析信道功率尾部行为，证明其从Gumbel域转移到Fréchet域；推导最大SINR的缩放规律，进而得到遍历容量缩放律；并推广至干扰受限的泊松网络模型以及MIMO随机波束赋形场景。

Result: 1) 最大SINR随用户数K呈K^{1/m_s}多项式缩放；2) 遍历容量缩放律为(1/m_s)log₂K；3) 在干扰受限网络中缩放指数不变，仅常数改变；4) 在m_s≤3的阴影衰落条件下，Fr échet渐近优于传统对数正态模型；5) 准静态阴影下比例公平调度会回退至Gumbel缩放。

Conclusion: 复合衰落下的多用户分集增益存在根本性缩放律转变，可为体域网、车联网/工业物联网及密集室内环境等场景提供显著高于经典理论预测的吞吐量增益。

Abstract: Classical multi-user diversity theory predicts that throughput over Rayleigh fading channels grows as $\log_2\!\log_2 K$. In this work, we demonstrate a fundamental shift in this scaling law under heavy-tailed composite fading. Specifically, under Fisher--Snedecor $\mathcal{F}$ composite fading, the channel power acquires a regularly varying upper tail, shifting extreme-value statistics from the Gumbel to the Fréchet domain. We prove that the maximum SINR among $K$ users scales polynomially as $K^{1/m_s}$, where $m_s$ is the shadowing severity parameter, leading to an ergodic capacity scaling of $\frac{1}{m_s}\log_2 K$. Crucially, this scaling persists in interference-limited Poisson networks, where aggregate co-channel interference alters the scaling constant but not the exponent. This polynomial gain is most relevant in severe-to-moderate shadowing ($m_s \le 3$), as encountered in body-area networks, vehicular/industrial IoT, and dense indoor environments, where Fréchet asymptotics overtake industry-standard lognormal models at practical user counts. Finally, we establish the conditions necessary to harvest this gain (showing that proportional-fair scheduling under quasi-static shadowing reverts to Gumbel scaling) and validate all analytical findings through Monte Carlo simulations, including MIMO random beamforming.

</details>


### [102] [A Criterion to Determine True Minimum Distances of Goppa Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23139)
*Shuying Dong,Hao Chen,Yaqi Chen,Ziyan Xie,Chengju Li*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对Goppa码达到设计距离的问题，建立了判别准则：对于特定形式的Goppa多项式，当且仅当满足所给条件时，对应Goppa码包含重量为t+1的码字。基于此准则，通过插值法构造Goppa多项式，推导出一系列达到设计距离的Goppa码族，并通过选取不同的辅助多项式V(x)和不可约多项式H(x)获得多个显式码族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Goppa码在基于编码的密码学（如McEliece密码系统）中具有重要作用，但其真实最小距离的确定是一个公认的难题。本文旨在建立Goppa码达到设计距离的判别准则，并构造达到设计距离的具体码族。

Method: 首先建立Goppa码包含重量为t+1码字的充要条件判别准则；然后基于该准则，发展插值法构造Goppa多项式；最后通过选取不同的辅助多项式V(x)（单项式、二项式及其乘积）和不可约多项式H(x)（不同形式的二项式和三项式），获得多个显式达到设计距离的Goppa码族。

Result: 建立了Goppa码达到设计距离的充要判别准则，基于此推导出一系列达到设计距离的Goppa码族，包括通过不同形式的V(x)和H(x)组合得到的多个显式码族。

Conclusion: 本文成功建立了判别Goppa码达到设计距离的准则，并通过插值方法构造了多个达到设计距离的Goppa码族，为Goppa码最小距离的确定提供了新的理论工具和构造方法。

Abstract: Goppa codes play an important role in code-based cryptography due to their efficient decoding algorithms and their use as underlying private codes in the McEliece cryptosystem. To determine the true minimum distances of Goppa codes is a notoriously difficult problem. In this paper, we establish a criterion for a Goppa code to attain its designed distance. We consider Goppa polynomials of the form $G(x)=U(x)H(x)+V(x)H'(x)$, where $°(G)=t$ and $H(x)\in\mathbb{F}_q[x]$ is a monic irreducible polynomial of degree $t+1$ whose roots are contained in the support $L$. We prove that the corresponding Goppa code $Γ(L,G)$ contains a codeword of weight $t+1$ if and only if \[ \frac{V(α_{i_{t+1}})}{V(α_{i_j})}\in\mathbb{F}_q^*, \qquad 1\leq j\leq t, \] where $α_{i_1},\ldots,α_{i_{t+1}}$ are the roots of $H(x)$. Based on this criterion, we derive a general family of Goppa codes that attain their designed distance by developing an interpolation-based construction of Goppa polynomials. We further obtain families of Goppa codes whose Goppa polynomials are determined by considering monomial, binomial, and their product of the auxiliary polynomial $V(x)$. By taking $H(x)$ to be different irreducible binomials and trinomials, we obtain several explicit families of Goppa codes whose minimum distances are equal to designed distance.

</details>


### [103] [Energy-Efficient Dual-Band Communication: How to Allocate Traffic to Sub-THz Carriers?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23205)
*Anders Enqvist,Emil Björnson,Cicek Cavdar*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究了双频基站（sub-6 GHz + sub-THz）的能效极限，通过联合优化硬件参数和休眠模式，推导出四个能效最优运行区域的闭合解析阈值，确定了sub-THz频段何时唤醒及流量分配策略，为绿色网络的功耗最小化提供了数学框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 6G网络向sub-THz频段演进以满足极端容量需求，但面临大带宽与功耗之间的权衡挑战，亟需系统性地研究双频基站站点的能效极限。

Method: 通过联合优化硬件参数和基于活动因子的高级休眠模式，推导出能效最优行为的四个不同运行区域及闭合解析阈值。

Result: 当带宽受限的sub-6 GHz频段的功率成本超过激活sub-THz电路的静态功耗代价时，使用sub-THz频段是能效最优的；给出了精确的唤醒阈值和流量分配策略。

Conclusion: 该框架为未来绿色网络的功耗最小化和休眠模式管理提供了数学上严谨的指导准则。

Abstract: As 6G wireless networks transition toward sub-Terahertz (sub-THz) frequencies to satisfy extreme capacity demands, managing the trade-off between massive bandwidth and power consumption becomes a critical design challenge. In this paper, we investigate the fundamental energy efficiency (EE) limits of a dual-band base station site combining a coverage-oriented sub-6 GHz carrier with a capacity-oriented sub-THz carrier. By jointly optimizing hardware parameters and advanced sleep modes via activity factors, we identify four distinct operational regions that govern the EE-optimal behavior across the complete range of data rates. We derive closed-form analytical thresholds that dictate precisely when the sub-THz band should awaken from sleep and how to allocate traffic between the bands in that case. Our results demonstrate that utilizing the sub-THz band is EE-optimal when the power cost of the bandwidth-limited sub-6 GHz band surpasses the static power penalty of activating the sub-THz circuitry. Ultimately, this framework provides mathematically rigorous guidelines for power consumption minimization and sleep-mode management in future green networks.

</details>


### [104] [Movable-Antenna Assisted Energy Minimization in UAV-Enabled Mobile Edge Computing Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23214)
*Jiang Chen,Chunjie Wang,Xuhui Zhang,Yanyan Shen,Kejiang Ye,Chengzhong Xu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出一种可移动天线（MA）辅助的无人机（UAV）移动边缘计算（MEC）系统能量最小化方案，通过联合优化计算资源分配、传输功率、接收波束成形和天线位置，利用块坐标下降法迭代求解非凸问题，显著降低了系统能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 延迟敏感型应用的指数级增长推动了MEC的发展，但降低MEC系统的高能耗仍然是一个关键挑战。本文旨在通过可移动天线技术进一步优化UAV-MEC系统的能耗。

Method: 提出基于块坐标下降法的鲁棒交替优化算法，将原问题迭代分解为三个子问题：计算资源分配、传输功率与接收波束成形（采用二次变换技术优化）、MA阵列位置优化（采用粒子群优化算法）。

Result: 数值仿真验证了所提方案相比传统基准方法实现了显著的能耗节省。

Conclusion: 可移动天线辅助的UAV-MEC系统通过联合优化各变量能够有效降低系统总能耗，为未来绿色MEC系统设计提供了新思路。

Abstract: Driven by the exponential growth of latency-sensitive applications, mobile edge computing (MEC) has emerged as a pivotal paradigm, yet mitigating its substantial energy consumption remains critical. This paper explores a movable-antenna (MA) assisted energy minimization scheme in an uncrewed aerial vehicle (UAV)-enabled MEC system, where a UAV equipped with an MA array serves as an edge server to process tasks offloaded from terrestrial consumer electronics (CE) devices. To minimize the total system energy consumption, we jointly optimize computation resource allocation, CE transmit power, receive beamforming, and MA positions. To tackle the resulting non-convex problem with coupled variables, a robust alternating optimization algorithm based on the block coordinate descent method is developed. The problem is iteratively decomposed into three subproblems. In particular, the subproblem of transmit power and receive beamforming is reformulated and optimized using the quadratic transform technique, while the MA array positions are optimized via the particle swarm optimization algorithm. Numerical simulations verify that the proposed scheme achieves substantial energy savings over conventional benchmarks.

</details>


### [105] [Exploiting Movable Antennas for Multi-Target Wireless Sensing System in Multipath Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23249)
*Xiang Chen,Ming-Min Zhao,Lebin Chen,Jie Xu,Min-Jian Zhao*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究可移动天线（MA）无线传感系统中的多目标检测问题，提出基于交叉稀疏马尔可夫混合先验的后验概率推导方法，以及二维模糊函数作为性能度量，并通过Dykstra投影梯度下降算法优化天线位置以抑制旁瓣、窄化主瓣，仿真验证了所提方法在性能和计算复杂度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统固定位置天线阵列在多目标多径场景下感知性能受限，而可移动天线通过自适应调整天线位置可重新配置传播几何结构，为复杂多径环境下的多目标检测提供额外设计自由度，有望显著提升感知性能。

Method: 1) 提出交叉稀疏马尔可夫混合先验模型，利用LoS与NLoS路径间的结构相关性推导目标位置的后验概率；2) 提出二维模糊函数作为多目标检测的角域感知性能度量指标；3) 建立非凸优化问题，设计基于Dykstra的投影梯度下降算法优化MA位置，以抑制旁瓣、窄化主瓣。

Result: 仿真验证了所提模糊函数分析的准确性，证明了所提先验模型和可移动天线带来的显著性能增益，且所提算法在达到与现有方法相当性能的同时，计算复杂度显著降低。

Conclusion: 可移动天线结合所提先验模型和优化算法能有效提升多目标多径场景下的感知性能，且所提Dykstra投影梯度下降算法在保证性能的同时具有低复杂度优势。

Abstract: In this paper, we study the multi-target detection problem in a movable-antenna (MA)-enabled wireless sensing system with linear arrays, in which both the direct line-of-sight (LoS) paths and the first-order non-LoS (NLoS) paths are explicitly considered. Unlike conventional fixed-position antenna arrays, MAs provide additional design degrees of freedom by enabling adaptive antenna positioning to reconfigure the propagation geometry, offering great potential to enhance the sensing performance in complex multi-target multipath scenarios. Under this setup, we first develop a cross sparsity Markov mixture prior to derive the posterior probabilities of target locations, in which the structural correlation between the LoS and NLoS paths is effectively exploited to enhance the location estimation accuracy. Based on the derived posterior probabilities, we further analyze the angular-domain sensing performance for multi-target detection by proposing a new two-dimensional (2D) ambiguity function as the performance metric. Next, we optimize the MA positions to suppress the sidelobe levels and narrow the mainlobe width of the proposed ambiguity function. Although the resulting problem is highly non-convex, we develop a low-complexity Dykstra-based projected gradient descent algorithm to solve it efficiently. Finally, simulation results verify the accuracy of the proposed ambiguity function analysis, demonstrate the substantial performance gains enabled by the proposed prior model and MAs, and show that the proposed algorithm achieves performance comparable to existing methods with significantly lower computational complexity.

</details>


### [106] [Game of Coding under Computation-Dependent Adversarial Noise](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23318)
*Hanzaleh Akbari Nodehi,Mohammad Ali Maddah-Ali*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究输入依赖型对抗噪声下的编码博弈，通过构造一个输入无关的联合噪声分布，证明其与原始输入依赖模型在接受概率、均方估计误差、可达性能区域和均衡效用上完全等价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码博弈框架假设对抗噪声分布与真实计算值独立，但这一假设在面对知情敌手（能根据被计算的值调整其报告）时具有局限性，因此需要研究输入依赖型对抗噪声下的编码博弈。

Method: 引入统一的多节点、多维编码博弈模型；针对每一类条件对抗噪声分布，构造一个输入无关的联合噪声分布，并证明该约化过程精确保持接受概率和接受的均方估计误差。

Result: 证明了输入依赖模型与输入无关模型具有相同的可达性能区域和相同的均衡效用。

Conclusion: 输入依赖型对抗噪声模型可通过构造等价输入无关噪声分布进行分析，两者在关键性能指标上完全等价。

Abstract: The game of coding framework was introduced to extend coding-theoretic recovery beyond its traditional limit, under which the number of honest reports must exceed the number of adversarial or corrupted reports. It does so by exploiting the rational behavior of adversarial participants and their incentive to keep the system live. Existing game-of-coding formulations, however, assume that the adversarial-noise distribution is independent of the realized ground-truth computation. This assumption may be restrictive when an informed adversary can adapt its reports to the value being computed.
  In this paper, we study the game of coding with input-dependent adversarial noise. We introduce a unified multi-node, multidimensional formulation. For every family of conditional adversarial-noise distributions, we construct an input-independent joint noise distribution, and prove that this reduction exactly preserves the probability of acceptance and the accepted mean-squared estimation error. Consequently, the input-dependent and input-independent models have identical achievable performance regions, and the same equilibrium utilities.

</details>


### [107] [Lossless Address Coding for Quantum Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23510)
*Dick Maryopi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于量子网络的无损源编码方案，通过前缀-后缀地址空间和等距层次编码器-解码器实现紧凑、层次化且可相干处理的量子地址状态，并基于霍夫曼编码嵌入前缀无关的长度本征态码字，在13节点网络示例中验证了可行性且实现了完美保真度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着量子系统向互联架构发展，需要能够标识节点、管理资源并支持网络级功能，但目前缺乏紧凑、层次化且可相干处理的量子地址编码方案。

Method: 提出前缀-后缀地址空间结构，设计等距层次编码器-解码器保证唯一可解码性；开发基于霍夫曼的实用程序，将前缀无关的长度本征态码字嵌入地址空间以保持等距性。

Result: 在13节点网络数值示例中，所提出的层次编码方案可行且实现了完美保真度。

Conclusion: 该工作在源编码理论与量子网络设计之间建立了严格联系，为可扩展且相干的量子寻址提供了实用框架。

Abstract: As quantum systems advance toward interconnected architectures, the ability to identify nodes, manage resources, and support network-level functions becomes increasingly critical. In this work, we propose a lossless source coding scheme for addressing in quantum networks that enables compact, hierarchical, and coherently processable quantum address states. Specifically, we introduce a prefix-suffix address space and develop an isometric hierarchical encoder-decoder that guarantees unique decodability. We further provide a practical Huffman-based procedure that embeds prefix-free, length-eigenstate codewords into the address space, thereby preserving isometry. The scheme is particularly designed for networks with hierarchical structure, heterogeneous cluster sizes, and configurable address assignment to accommodate dynamic network conditions. A numerical example on a 13-node network demonstrates that the proposed hierarchical encoding scheme is feasible and achieves perfect fidelity. This work establishes a rigorous connection between source coding theory and quantum network design, offering a practical framework towards scalable and coherent quantum addressing.

</details>


### [108] [Optimality of Bang-Bang Switching for Breaking the Chu Limit via Time-Modulated Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23688)
*Mohamed Akrout,Miguel Rodrigo Castellanos*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了通过时间调制匹配超越Chu极限需要非光滑的开关策略。作者推导了一个非线性微分条件，表明可微调制函数是次优的，并证明了最大化超越Chu极限的最优开关轨迹是分段常数（Bang-Bang）轮廓。最后，建立了连接天线尺寸、开关速度和误码率的上界，揭示了当天线电尺寸变小时，开关时间变长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究通过时间调制匹配方式超越天线基本物理极限（Chu极限）的可能性，并寻找最优的调制策略。

Method: 推导非线性微分条件证明可微调制函数的次优性，采用Bang-Bang（分段常数）控制策略作为最优开关轨迹，建立天线尺寸、开关速度与误码率之间的上界关系。

Result: 发现可微调制函数在超越Chu极限时是次优的，最优策略是Bang-Bang轮廓；并且天线电尺寸越小，所需的开关时间越长。

Conclusion: 超越Chu极限必须以非光滑（非连续可微）的开关方式实现，且电小天线的时间调制匹配面临更严格的开关速度约束。

Abstract: This paper shows that surpassing the Chu limit on $Q$-factors via time-modulated matching requires non-smooth switching strategies. We derive a nonlinear differential condition to show that differentiable modulation functions are sub-optimal, then show that the optimal switching trajectory for maximizing the violation of the Chu limit is a piecewise-constant (Bang-Bang) profile. Finally, we establish an upper-bound connecting the antenna size, switching speed, and bit error rate, which reveals that switching time becomes longer as antennas become electrically smaller.

</details>


### [109] [The automorphism groups of random linear codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.23936)
*Xiaoru Li,Qi Wang,Yue Zhou*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文证明了当最小维度满足条件时，随机线性码的自同构群以高概率是平凡的，填补了匹配码字框架中一个长期未正式证明的假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 匹配码字框架是分析基于线性码等价问题（如LESS签名方案）密码方案安全性的标准工具，该框架依赖于q元随机码具有平凡自同构群的假设，但这一假设在文献中从未被正式证明。

Method: 采用概率分析方法，研究当n趋向无穷大时，k维随机码C ⊆ F_q^n的自同构群的平凡性，设定条件min{k, n-k} ≥ (2+ε)log_q n（任意ε>0）。

Result: 证明了在上述条件下，随机线性码的自同构群以高概率是平凡的。

Conclusion: 该结果正式验证了匹配码字框架中的关键假设，为基于LCE问题的密码方案（如LESS）提供了更坚实的安全性理论基础。

Abstract: The study of automorphism groups of linear codes is a fundamental topic in coding theory. The matching codewords framework is currently a standard tool for analyzing the security of cryptographic schemes based on the hardness of the Linear Code Equivalence (LCE) problem, such as the LESS signature scheme. This framework often relies on the assumption that $q$-ary random codes have trivial automorphism groups. However, this assumption has not been formally proved in the literature. In this paper, we prove that with high probability, $k$-dimensional random codes $\mathcal{C} \subseteq \mathbb{F}_q^n$ have a trivial automorphism group as $n$ goes to infinity as long as $\min\{k, n-k\} \geq (2+\varepsilon)\log_q n$, for any $\varepsilon >0$.

</details>


### [110] [Beam Training for RIS-Aided ISAC Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24003)
*Jinho Yang,Hyeongtaek Lee,Junil Choi*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对RIS辅助的ISAC系统，提出了一种低开销的波束训练框架，可在通信UE波束对齐的同时通过回波信号检测目标；采用5G标准码本的部分搜索策略确定合适码字组合，利用辅助波束对方法获取角度信息，并基于角度估计实现高精度闭式定位，且扩展至多目标场景。仿真验证了所提方法在训练效率和定位精度上的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RIS可为ISAC提供额外自由度以增强通信与感知性能，但现有研究中缺乏高效的波束训练方案来同时实现通信UE的波束对齐和目标检测，因此需要设计低训练开销的联合框架。

Method: 基于5G标准码本原则构建码本，提出部分搜索过程实现低开销波束训练；利用辅助波束对方法从基站和RIS两个视角获取目标角度信息；基于角度估计提出高精度闭式定位方法，并扩展至多目标定位场景。

Result: 数值结果表明，所提训练过程能有效找到合适码字组合，且目标定位技术优于基准方法。

Conclusion: 所提波束训练框架可在低开销下同时实现通信UE波束对齐与目标检测，基于角度估计的闭式定位方法在ISAC场景中具有优势，并能有效扩展至多目标场景。

Abstract: As a key technology for 6G, integrated sensing and communication (ISAC) is receiving considerable attention, and deploying a reconfigurable intelligent surface (RIS) can enhance both communication performance and sensing capability of ISAC by providing additional degrees of freedom. In this paper, we investigate a beam training framework for RIS-aided ISAC systems where beam alignment for a communication user equipment (UE) is conducted while simultaneously detecting a single target through its echo signal. Using codebooks constructed according to the principles of the 5G standard, we propose a partial search procedure that achieves low training overhead and mathematically show that this strategy is sufficient to identify a suitable codeword combination to serve the UE. By applying the auxiliary beam pair method, the target's angle information from the perspectives of the base station and RIS is obtained. Then, a high-accuracy closed-form localization is proposed based on the angle estimates, and we further extend the proposed technique to multi-target localization scenarios. Numerical results highlight the advantages of the proposed technique in the ISAC context, showing that the training procedure can effectively find a codeword combination and that the target localization technique outperforms the benchmarks.

</details>


### [111] [Secrecy Energy Efficiency for IRS-Assisted Low-Altitude Communications: A D3QN-PER Based Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24183)
*Ya Gao,Peina Zhao,Yiheng Li,Wenchi Cheng*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文针对低空经济（LAE）无线通信中的安全与能效挑战，提出了一种无人机（UAV）与智能反射面（IRS）协同的保密能效（SEE）最大化方案。通过Dinkelbach方法、交替优化和D3QN-PER算法联合优化波束赋形、IRS相位和无人机轨迹，仿真验证了所提方案在提升SEE方面的优越性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低空经济无线通信面临安全性和能量效率的双重挑战，现有方法难以同时满足二者需求。

Method: 建立了UAV-IRS辅助LAE通信的信道传输模型；将SEE最大化问题转化为非凸分式优化问题；利用Dinkelbach方法和等价变换重构目标函数；通过交替优化策略将问题解耦为三个独立子问题；采用松弛变量和半定松弛（SDR）凸化波束赋形和IRS相位优化子问题；针对无人机轨迹优化，提出了D3QN-PER算法（集成Dueling Double DQN与优先经验回放）解决传统DQN收敛慢和训练不稳定的问题。

Result: 数值仿真验证了联合优化方案的有效性，D3QN-PER算法在提升SEE方面优于现有最先进的学习方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的UAV-IRS协同安全通信框架及D3QN-PER优化算法能有效提升低空经济无线通信网络的保密能效。

Abstract: To address the security and energy efficiency challenges in low-altitude economy (LAE) wireless communications, we develop a secure synergistic network integrating unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) and intelligent reflecting surface (IRS), with an emphasis on maximizing secrecy energy efficiency (SEE) for downlink transmission scenarios. In particular, firstly, we establish the channel transmission models for UAV-IRS assisted LAE communications network. Then, we formulate a non-convex fractional optimization problem for SEE maximization, involving three tightly coupled variables, i.e., the beamforming, IRS phase and UAV trajectory. To tackle the fractional structure and variable coupling, Dinkelbach's method and equivalent transformations are leveraged to reformulate the objective function, which is then decoupled and decomposed into three independent subproblems via an alternating optimization strategy for iterative resolution. Slack variables and Semidefinite Relaxation (SDR) are further employed to convexify the subproblems of beamforming and IRS phase shift optimization, thereby obtaining their optimal solutions. For the UAV trajectory optimization subproblem, we propose a D3QN-PER algorithm, which integrates a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network with Prioritized Experience Replay, to tackle the slow convergence and training instability inherent in conventional Deep Q-Network (DQN). Numerical simulations validate the performance for our proposed joint optimization scheme. Comparative results demonstrate that the developed D3QN-PER-based algorithm outperforms existing state-of-the-art learning approaches which verifies its superiority in improving SEE for UAV-IRS-assisted LAE wireless communications network.

</details>


### [112] [LLM-based Source Code Compression via Thresholded Symbol Ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24192)
*Angelo Nardone,Paolo Ferragina*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的源代码无损压缩方法，通过Top-T符号排名变体（T=1或63）限制预测范围，将超出阈值的符号作为异常存储并与排名流联合压缩。在30个LLM上的大规模评估显示，该方法在压缩比（相对提升最高37%）和压缩吞吐量（快40%）上均优于此前LLM压缩器，相比通用压缩器（zstd、bzip2）最高获得82%的压缩增益，且代码压缩增益强于自然语言，表明LLM能捕捉通用压缩器无法利用的源代码规律。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模软件存档（如Software Heritage）对源代码存储需求巨大，通用压缩器无法充分利用源代码中的特殊规律性；现有基于LLM的压缩方法（Shannon符号排名框架）虽效果更好，但预测排名可任意增长导致吞吐量显著下降，且是否需要显式编码所有排名仍是开放问题。

Method: 提出两种Top-T符号排名变体（T=1或63）：将LLM预测限制在前T个排名中，超出阈值的符号作为异常单独存储，再与排名流一同通过通用压缩器联合压缩，从而实现有界的编码开销。

Result: 在30个LLM（通用、代码专用、量化模型）上的评估中，Top-T方法相比此前LLM压缩器压缩比提升最高37%，吞吐量提升40%；相比zstd、bzip2等通用压缩器压缩增益最高达82%；代码压缩增益显著高于自然语言。

Conclusion: Top-T有界排名方法在压缩比和速度之间提供了新的权衡点，源代码展现出LLM可捕获但通用精确匹配压缩器遗漏的规律性，最后指出了理论和实践上的开放问题。

Abstract: We study the problem of lossless compression of source code, motivated by the storage demands of large-scale software archives, such as Software Heritage (https://www.softwareheritage.org/). General-purpose compressors (e.g., zstd, bzip2) offer a good trade-off between compression ratio and speed, but fail to exploit all special regularities inherent in source code. Recent approaches leverage Large Language Models (LLMs) within Shannon's symbol-ranking framework, relying on a scheme in which the predicted rank can grow arbitrarily. While effective at reducing space, this setting incurs significant throughput degradation, and leaves open the question whether it is necessary to explicitly encode all ranks. In this work, we introduce LLM-based compressors deploying two novel symbol-ranking variants that bound predictions to the top-$T$ ranks ($T=1$ or $63$), with out-of-threshold symbols stored as exceptions and compressed jointly with the rank stream via general-purpose compressors. We conduct the first large-scale evaluation of LLM-based source code compression across 30 LLMs, including general-domain, code-specialized, and quantized models. Our $T$-bounded approach outperforms prior LLM-based compressors both in compression ratio (up to 37% relative improvement) and compression throughput (40% faster). Compared to general-purpose compressors (e.g., zstd, bzip2), we obtain up to 82% relative compression gain but at a lower speed, thus offering a new trade-off point in the compression-speed spectrum. We also show that these gains are stronger on source code than on natural language, suggesting an interesting indication, namely that source code exposes regularities captured by LLMs but missed by general-purpose exact-match-based compressors. We conclude by commenting on open problems that offer theoretical and practical avenues of research.

</details>


### [113] [Minimum enclosing Bregman balls made easy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24197)
*Frank Nielsen*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文重新审视了最小包围Bregman球(Bregman MEB)问题，证明了Bregman MEB等价于对应加权点集在幂距离下的MEB，提出了高效的Frank-Wolfe (1+ε)-近似算法，并揭示了Bregman势提升变换与经典抛物面提升变换之间的等价关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重新审视并深入理解最小包围Bregman球(Bregman MEB)问题的理论基础，建立其与幂距离MEB、Frank-Wolfe算法以及Bregman Voronoi图之间的深层联系。

Method: 首先证明Bregman MEB与加权点集在幂距离下的MEB等价；然后提出基于Frank-Wolfe的(1+ε)-近似算法用于计算幂距离MEB；最后通过理论分析揭示Bregman势提升变换与经典抛物面提升变换的等价性。

Result: 建立了Bregman MEB与幂距离MEB的等价关系；给出了高效的Frank-Wolfe近似算法；证明了Bregman势提升变换可重新解释为经典抛物面提升变换作用于对应加权点集；Bregman MEB的外心位于最远Bregman Voronoi图或等价的最远幂图上。

Conclusion: Bregman MEB问题可以通过幂距离框架统一处理，Frank-Wolfe算法提供了高效近似求解途径，且Bregman几何与经典欧氏几何中的提升变换存在深刻的理论联系。

Abstract: In this work, we revisit the problem of computing minimum enclosing Bregman balls (Bregman MEBs) of finite sets of parameters. First, we show that Bregman MEBs are equivalent to MEBs of corresponding weighted point sets with respect to the power distance. We then report an efficient Frank--Wolfe $(1+ε)$-approximation algorithm for computing power MEBs, for any $ε>0$. This power MEB approximation algorithm coincides with the Bregman MEB approximation algorithm of Nock and Nielsen (2005) when expressed in the dual gradient space. Finally, we show that the Bregman potential lifting transforms used to construct Bregman Voronoi diagrams can be reinterpreted as the classical paraboloid lifting transform applied to corresponding weighted point sets. In particular, Bregman MEB circumcenters lie on the farthest Bregman Voronoi diagrams or equivalently on the corresponding farthest power diagrams.

</details>


### [114] [Where to Perform Channel Measurements for CKM Construction: A Random Field Theory Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24283)
*Yuxuan Song,Yong Zeng,Xiping Wu,Guangchi Zhang,Cheng-Xiang Wang,Rui Zhang*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文研究面向数据驱动信道知识地图（CKM）构建的空间测量策略，以信道增益图（CGM）为例，通过离散化空间并优化测量位置选择，提出自适应离散化方法以最小化全局CGM构建的平均均方误差（AMSE），相比均匀离散化取得了显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据驱动CKM构建依赖于空间相关性原理，但面临"在哪里进行信道测量"的关键问题。如何以有限的测量数据高效构建全局CKM，需要科学的空间测量策略来指导测量位置的选择。

Method: 将连续物理空间离散化为有限立方体网格点，将测量位置选择建模为组合优化问题；采用贪婪算法和模拟退火（SA）两种代表性算法求解；并从高斯随机场理论出发，提出自适应离散化策略，以最小化原始连续场到离散表示在均方意义上的信息损失。

Result: 相比均匀离散化，所提自适应离散化策略在减小平均均方误差（AMSE）方面取得了显著的性能增益。

Conclusion: 建立了空间测量的理论框架，为数据驱动CKM的高效构建提供了实用指导，克服了传统均匀离散化在精度与复杂度之间的权衡问题。

Abstract: Channel knowledge map (CKM) is regarded as a promising technology for future sixth-generation (6G) networks, facilitating environmental-aware wireless communication, sensing, and localization. Research works on CKM construction can be classified as model-based methods and data-based approaches. Specifically, data-based CKM construction exploits the fundamental principle of spatial correlation to complete CKM based on limited measurement data, leading to the question of "where to perform channel measurements". In this paper, we study the spatial measurement strategy for efficient data-based CKM construction, and consider a specific type of CKM named channel gain map (CGM). The general objective is to select a subset of locations for channel measurements, so as to minimize the average mean-squared-error (AMSE) of the global CGM construction. In order to reduce the infinite measurement locations to a finite set, we discretize the underlying physical space into a finite number of cubic grid points, and formulate a combinatorial optimization problem to select measurement locations from them. In order to solve the proposed problem, we employ two representative algorithms, namely the greedy algorithm and the simulated annealing (SA), and discuss their respective advantages. To overcome the accuracy-complexity trade-off of traditional uniform discretization, we develop an adaptive discretization strategy from the viewpoint of Gaussian random field theory to minimize the information loss from the original continuous field to its approximated discrete representation in the mean-squared sense. Compared to uniform discretization, the proposed adaptive discretization strategy achieves a significant performance gain in terms of AMSE-reduction, establishing the theoretical framework of spatial measurement and providing practical guidance for implementation.

</details>


### [115] [The Semantic Least-Energy Principle: A Hypothesis for Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24287)
*Jie Zhang,Haoyuan Zhu,James Jinheng Zhang,Haonan Hu*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出语义最小能量原理（SLEP），认为智能系统通过最大化语义效用同时最小化语义、预测和计算能量来演化内部表征，并在变分框架中将语义认知建模为语义作用泛函的驻点解，从而统一解释语义抽象、推理、规划和通信等现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有框架（信息论、信息瓶颈、预测编码、自由能原理等）虽能解释通信、学习和预测，但未能从第一性原理层面解释语义智能为何以特定方式组织潜在语义状态。本文旨在提出一个统一的第一性原理来填补这一空白。

Method: 采用变分框架，定义语义作用泛函（Semantic Action Functional），其驻点解对应潜在语义流形上的高效轨迹；通过最大化语义效用并最小化语义能量、预测能量和计算能量来推导语义几何、语义热力学和低能潜在语义状态等理论预测。

Result: 推导出系列理论预测：语义几何（潜在语义空间的结构性质）、语义热力学（语义状态的能量-熵关系）以及低能潜在语义状态（作为优化过程的互补结果）。SLEP 能够统一解释语义抽象、推理、规划和通信。

Conclusion: SLEP 为理解语义智能提供了一个潜在的第一性原理基础，能够生成对人工和生物智能均可实验检验的假设，但尚需严格验证。

Abstract: Despite remarkable advances in artificial intelligence and cognitive neuroscience, no generally accepted first-principle explains why intelligent systems organize latent semantic states as they do. Existing frameworks such as information theory, the Information Bottleneck, the Degree of Information Abstraction, predictive coding and the Free Energy Principle provide powerful frameworks for understanding communication, learning, and prediction, but do not explicitly explain the emergence and organization of semantic intelligence. Here we propose the \textbf{Semantic Least-Energy Principle (SLEP)} as a hypothesis that intelligent systems evolve internal representations by maximizing semantic utility while progressively minimizing semantic, predictive, and computational energy. We formulate this hypothesis within a variational framework in which semantic cognition is governed by a Semantic Action Functional whose stationary solutions define efficient trajectories on a latent semantic manifold. This formulation emerges a series of theoretical predictions, including semantic geometry, semantic thermodynamics, and low-energy latent semantic states as complementary consequences of the same underlying optimization process. SLEP unifies semantic abstraction, reasoning, planning, and communication within a common mathematical framework while generating experimentally testable predictions for both artificial and biological intelligence. Although the hypothesis remains to be rigorously validated, it provides a principled foundation for investigating semantic intelligence from a first-principle perspective.

</details>


### [116] [New perspectives for code locality in the rank metric](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.24295)
*Camille Garnier,Julien Lavauzelle,Jade Nardi,Ilaria Zappatore*

Main category: cs.IT

TL;DR: 本文提出了秩度量码中局部恢复的新定义，与Kadhe等人的前期工作不同，新定义允许高效恢复支撑集的任意元素，不依赖基的选择，并给出了Singleton-like界及最优构造。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
